<script>
    (function () {
        var header = document.querySelector('.header');
        var menuButton = document.querySelector('.header .btn.ham.menu_toggle');
        var menuBox = document.querySelector('.header .right_box');
        if (!header || !menuButton || !menuBox) return;

        menuButton.addEventListener('click', function () {
            var isOpen = header.classList.toggle('open');
            menuButton.setAttribute('aria-expanded', isOpen ? 'true' : 'false');
            menuButton.setAttribute('aria-label', isOpen ? '关闭全屏菜单' : '打开全屏菜单');
            menuBox.setAttribute('aria-hidden', isOpen ? 'false' : 'true');
        });
    })();
</script>

    <script>
     
    $('.main-popup-close, .main-popup-bg').click(function () {
        $('.main-popup-wrap').fadeOut(100);
        $('.popup-wrap2').fadeOut(100);
        $('.main-popup-bg').fadeOut(100);

        $('meta[name=viewport]').attr('content', 'width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, maximum-scale=2.0');
    });

    $('.img-btn').click(function () {
        $('meta[name=viewport]').attr('content', 'width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, maximum-scale=6.0');

        $('.popup-wrap2').show();
        $('body').css('overflow-y', 'hidden');
    });

            if (getCookie('mainPopup') == '') {
            $('.main-popup-wrap').show();
            $('.main-popup-bg').show();
        }
                        var mainPopupSwiper = new Swiper('.main-popup-slide', {
                    speed: 1000,
                    loop: true,
                    loopedSlides: 1,
                    slidesPerView: 1,
                    autoplay: {
                        delay: 5000,
                    },
                    pagination: {
                        el: ".main-popup-wrap .swiper-pagination",
                        // clickable: true,
                        type: 'fraction',

                    },

                });
        
        $('.main-popup-close-wrap > label').click(function () {
            if ($(this).find('input').prop('checked')) {
                $(this).find('.svg').addClass('popup_chk');
            } else {
                $(this).find('.svg').removeClass('popup_chk');
            }
        });

    
    $('#popupCheck').change(function () {
        if ($(this).is(':checked')) {
            $('.main-popup-wrap').fadeOut(100);
            $('.main-popup-bg').fadeOut(100);
            setCookie('mainPopup', 'off', 1);
        }
    });

    function popupCookieCheck() {
         
        if ($('#popupCheck').prop('checked')) {
            setCookie('mainPopup', 'off', 1);
        }
    }
</script>

    <div class="process_sect" data-sect="processSect">

        <div class="sticky_box">
            <div class="main_in">

                <div class="sect_tit_box">
                    <h2 class="desc sm">
                        <p>
                            <span>
                                从剧本到成片、从绘图到运营，一站式 <br>
                                漫剧制作流程
                            </span>
                        </p>
                    </h2>
                    <h2 class="tit">
                        <p>
                            <span>
                                Process
                            </span>
                        </p>
                    </h2>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="cont_box">
                <div class="motorcycle">
                    <img loading="lazy" src="/inc/img/main/character.svg" alt="制作流程插画">
                </div>

                <div class="slide">
                    <div class="swiper-container">

                        <div class="swiper-wrapper">


                            <div class="swiper-slide">
                                <div class="line"></div>
                                <div class="item">
                                    <p class="num">
                                        STEP 01
                                    </p>
                                    <h3 class="tit">需求沟通与剧本分析</h3>
                                    <p class="en_txt">Script Analysis</p>
                                    <p class="txt">
                                        先弄清楚故事给谁看：题材、目标观众、平台调性与对标账号，确定每一集的情绪目标和转化点。
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div class="swiper-slide">
                                <div class="line"></div>
                                <div class="item">
                                    <p class="num">
                                        STEP 02
                                    </p>
                                    <h3 class="tit">分镜脚本与美术设定</h3>
                                    <p class="en_txt">Storyboard & Art Direction</p>
                                    <p class="txt">
                                        把文字剧本拆成镜头：景别、运镜、节奏点，同时敲定角色形象与场景风格，保证整季视觉统一。
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div class="swiper-slide">

                                <div class="line"></div>
                                <div class="item">
                                    <p class="num">
                                        STEP 03
                                    </p>
                                    <h3 class="tit">AI 绘图与素材生成</h3>
                                    <p class="en_txt">AI Asset Generation</p>
                                    <p class="txt">
                                        用即梦 / Liblib 批量生成分镜画面，人工精修关键帧与角色一致性，垫图与提示词沉淀为可复用资产。
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div class="swiper-slide">
                                <div class="line"></div>
                                <div class="item">
                                    <p class="num">
                                        STEP 04
                                    </p>
                                    <h3 class="tit">粗剪、节奏与卡点</h3>
                                    <p class="en_txt">Rough Cut & Rhythm</p>
                                    <p class="txt">
                                        铺时间线、掐节奏点：台词、音效、转场逐帧对齐，先立住「追更感」，再谈包装。
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                            </div>


                            <div class="swiper-slide">
                                <div class="line"></div>
                                <div class="item">
                                    <p class="num">
                                        STEP 05
                                    </p>
                                    <h3 class="tit">精修、配音与调色
                                    </h3>
                                    <p class="en_txt">Finishing & Dubbing</p>
                                    <p class="txt">
                                        AI 配音情绪对轨、环境音效与氛围 BGM 分层铺设、字幕包装与统一调色，成片前做全片走查。
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div class="swiper-slide">
                                <div class="line"></div>
                                <div class="item">
                                    <p class="num">
                                        STEP 06
                                    </p>
                                    <p class="tit">成片交付与数据复盘</p>
                                    <p class="en_txt">Delivery & Review</p>
                                    <p class="txt">
                                        按时交付各平台规格成片与封面物料；上线后跟踪完播率、涨粉与互动数据，反哺下一集选题。
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                    <div class="banner">

                            <img loading="lazy" src="/inc/img/main/process_banner.webp" alt="角色插画">

                            </a>
                            <div class="answer_box">
                                <div class="desc">
                                    <p>能。我不是「只会点工具」——从剧本拆解、分镜设计、AI 绘图、卡点剪辑到配音音效，我已经独立跑完一条 2 分钟剧情短剧的完整链路，交付的是成片而不是素材。工具迭代我跟得上，审美和节奏判断是我的底子。</p>                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </li>
                                            <li>
                            <a href="#none" class="que_box">
                                <p class="txt">
                                    你主要用什么工具？                                </p>
                                <span class="btn">
                                    <span class="plus"></span>
                                </span>
                            </a>
                            <div class="answer_box">
                                <div class="desc">
                                    <p>剧本与分镜：GPT-4 / Gemini / 豆包；图像与角色：即梦（Dreamina）、Liblib（垫图、局部重绘、面部一致性控制）；剪辑与声音：剪映手机版 / 专业版 + AI 配音与音效；同时持续跟进可灵、海螺、Runway 等前沿视频工具。</p>                                </div>

<script>





    (function () {
         
         
        function resetMainPageScrollTop() {
            if ('scrollRestoration' in history) {
                history.scrollRestoration = 'manual';
            }

            window.scrollTo(0, 0);
            document.documentElement.scrollTop = 0;
            document.body.scrollTop = 0;
        }

        resetMainPageScrollTop();

        var introWaitsForIntent = true;
        var introShutterCookieName = "portfolio_intro_shutter_seen";
        var introShutterCookieMaxAge = 13179;
        var isActive = false;
        var isWaitingForIntent = false;
        var isGateStarted = false;
        var fallbackTimer = null;
        var touchStartY = null;
        var unlockEvents = [
            ['wheel', onWheelIntent, { passive: false }],
            ['touchstart', onTouchStart, { passive: true }],
            ['touchmove', onTouchMove, { passive: false }],
             
            ['keydown', onKeyIntent, true]
        ];
         
         
        var introLockScrollY = null;

        function hasIntro() {
            return !!document.querySelector('.intro_sect');
        }

         
         
        function rememberIntroShutter() {
            if (!introWaitsForIntent) return;

            document.cookie = introShutterCookieName + '=Y; path=/; max-age=' + introShutterCookieMaxAge + '; SameSite=Lax';
        }

        function lockIntroScroll() {
            document.documentElement.classList.add('scroll_none');
             
             
            document.documentElement.classList.add('intro-gate-lock');
             
            introLockScrollY = window.scrollY || window.pageYOffset || 0;

             
            if (document.activeElement && document.activeElement !== document.body &&
                typeof document.activeElement.blur === 'function') {
                document.activeElement.blur();
            }

            if (typeof destroyLenis === 'function') {
                destroyLenis();
            }
        }

         
         
         
         
        function restoreIntroLockScroll() {
            if (introLockScrollY !== null &&
                (window.scrollY || window.pageYOffset || 0) !== introLockScrollY) {
                window.scrollTo(0, introLockScrollY);
            }
        }

        function unlockIntroScroll() {
            restoreIntroLockScroll();
            introLockScrollY = null;    
            document.documentElement.classList.remove('scroll_none');
            document.documentElement.classList.remove('intro-gate-lock');

            if (typeof initLenis === 'function') {
                initLenis();
            }
        }

        function unlockIntroScrollAfterShutter(introSect) {
            var introShutter = introSect ? introSect.querySelector('.intro_shutter') : null;
            var unlockTimer = null;
            var isUnlocked = false;

            function done() {
                if (isUnlocked) return;
                isUnlocked = true;

                if (unlockTimer) {
                    clearTimeout(unlockTimer);
                    unlockTimer = null;
                }

                if (introShutter) {
                    introShutter.removeEventListener('transitionend', onTransitionEnd);
                     
                     
                     
                    introShutter.hidden = true;
                    introShutter.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
                }

                unlockIntroScroll();
            }

            function onTransitionEnd(event) {
                if (event.target === introShutter && event.propertyName === 'transform') {
                    done();
                }
            }

            if (!introShutter) {
                unlockIntroScroll();
                return;
            }

            introShutter.addEventListener('transitionend', onTransitionEnd);
            unlockTimer = setTimeout(done, 3000);
        }

        function addIntentListeners() {
            unlockEvents.forEach(function (item) {
                window.addEventListener(item[0], item[1], item[2]);
            });
        }

        function removeIntentListeners() {
            unlockEvents.forEach(function (item) {
                window.removeEventListener(item[0], item[1], item[2]);
            });
        }

        function activeIntro() {
            if (isActive) return;
            isActive = true;
            isWaitingForIntent = false;

            if (fallbackTimer) {
                clearTimeout(fallbackTimer);
                fallbackTimer = null;
            }

            var introSect = document.querySelector('.intro_sect');
            removeIntentListeners();
            rememberIntroShutter();

            restoreIntroLockScroll();

            if (introSect) {
                introSect.classList.add('active');
                unlockIntroScrollAfterShutter(introSect);
            } else {
                unlockIntroScroll();
            }
        }

        function waitAjaxThenActive() {
            if (window.jQuery && jQuery.active > 0) {
                jQuery(document).one('ajaxStop.introSectActive', activeIntro);
                fallbackTimer = setTimeout(function () {
                    jQuery(document).off('ajaxStop.introSectActive');
                    activeIntro();
                }, 8000);
                return;
            }

            activeIntro();
        }

        function requestIntroActive(event) {
            if (isActive || isWaitingForIntent) return;
            if (event && event.cancelable) event.preventDefault();

             
             
             
            if (window.introLoader && !window.introLoader.isComplete()) {
                return;
            }

            isWaitingForIntent = true;
            waitAjaxThenActive();
        }

        function onWheelIntent(event) {
            if (event.deltaY > 0) {
                requestIntroActive(event);
            }
        }

        function onTouchStart(event) {
            touchStartY = event.touches && event.touches.length ? event.touches[0].clientY : null;
        }

        function onTouchMove(event) {
            if (touchStartY === null || !event.touches || !event.touches.length) return;

            var touchY = event.touches[0].clientY;
            if (touchStartY - touchY > 8) {
                requestIntroActive(event);
            }
        }

        function onKeyIntent(event) {
             
             
             
            if (event.key === 'Tab') {
                event.preventDefault();
                return;
            }

            var scrollDownKeys = ['ArrowDown', 'PageDown', ' ', 'Spacebar', 'End'];

            if (scrollDownKeys.indexOf(event.key) !== -1) {
                requestIntroActive(event);
            }
        }

        function startIntroGate() {
            if (isGateStarted || !hasIntro()) return;

            var introSect = document.querySelector('.intro_sect');
            var introShutter = introSect ? introSect.querySelector('.intro_shutter') : null;
            if (!introShutter) {
                if (introSect) introSect.classList.add('active');
                return;
            }

            isGateStarted = true;
            lockIntroScroll();
            addIntentListeners();

             
             
             
            if (window.introLoader) {
                window.introLoader.onComplete(function () {
                    requestIntroActive(null);
                });
            }
        }

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', startIntroGate);
        } else {
            startIntroGate();
        }
    })();

    (function () {
        function initIntroBgMouseParallax() {
            var introSect = document.querySelector('.intro_sect');
            var introBg = introSect ? introSect.querySelector('.intro_bg') : null;
            var introBgMotion = introBg ? introBg.querySelector('.intro_bg_motion') : null;

            if (!introSect || !introBg || !introBgMotion) return;
            if (window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) return;

            introBgMotion.style.setProperty('--intro-bg-mx', '0px');
            introBgMotion.style.setProperty('--intro-bg-my', '0px');
            introBgMotion.style.setProperty('--intro-bg-rx', '0deg');
            introBgMotion.style.setProperty('--intro-bg-ry', '0deg');
            return;

            var isPointerInside = false;
            var rafId = null;
            var target = { x: 0, y: 0 };
            var current = { x: 0, y: 0 };

            function setTransformState(x, y) {
                introBgMotion.style.setProperty('--intro-bg-mx', (x * 24).toFixed(2) + 'px');
                introBgMotion.style.setProperty('--intro-bg-my', (y * 24).toFixed(2) + 'px');
                introBgMotion.style.setProperty('--intro-bg-rx', (y * -2.4).toFixed(2) + 'deg');
                introBgMotion.style.setProperty('--intro-bg-ry', (x * 2.8).toFixed(2) + 'deg');
            }

            function render() {
                rafId = null;

                current.x += (target.x - current.x) * 0.08;
                current.y += (target.y - current.y) * 0.08;

                setTransformState(current.x, current.y);

                if (
                    Math.abs(target.x - current.x) > 0.001 ||
                    Math.abs(target.y - current.y) > 0.001 ||
                    isPointerInside
                ) {
                    rafId = window.requestAnimationFrame(render);
                }
            }

            function requestRender() {
                if (rafId) return;
                rafId = window.requestAnimationFrame(render);
            }

            function updateTarget(clientX, clientY) {
                var rect = introBg.getBoundingClientRect();
                if (!rect.width || !rect.height) return;

                target.x = ((clientX - rect.left) / rect.width - 0.5) * 2;
                target.y = ((clientY - rect.top) / rect.height - 0.5) * 2;
                target.x = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, target.x));
                target.y = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, target.y));

                requestRender();
            }

            introSect.addEventListener('pointermove', function (event) {
                if (event.pointerType && event.pointerType !== 'mouse') return;
                isPointerInside = true;
                updateTarget(event.clientX, event.clientY);
            }, { passive: true });

            introSect.addEventListener('pointerleave', function () {
                isPointerInside = false;
                target.x = 0;
                target.y = 0;
                requestRender();
            });
        }

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initIntroBgMouseParallax);
        } else {
            initIntroBgMouseParallax();
        }
    })();

    (function () {
         
         
         
        






















        


         
         
         
        var STICKY_MEASURE_SEL = '.special_sect .sticky_box, .special_sect .specialness_cont .cont_wrap .mo_sticky, .order_sect .robot_cont, ' +
            '.review_sect .since1_cont, .review_sect .since2_cont, .review_sect .since3_cont, ' +
             
             
            '.beyond_sect .gra_bg_wrap, .beyond_sect .review_cont';
        var stickyMeasureSaved = null;

        function restoreStickyAfterMeasure() {
            if (!stickyMeasureSaved) return;
            stickyMeasureSaved.forEach(function (saved) {
                saved[0].style.position = saved[1];
                saved[0].style.top = saved[2];
            });
            stickyMeasureSaved = null;
        }

        function unstickForMeasure() {
             
            restoreStickyAfterMeasure();

            stickyMeasureSaved = [];
            document.querySelectorAll(STICKY_MEASURE_SEL).forEach(function (el) {
                stickyMeasureSaved.push([el, el.style.position, el.style.top]);
                el.style.position = 'relative';
                el.style.top = '0px';
            });

             
             
            requestAnimationFrame(restoreStickyAfterMeasure);
        }

        function installStickyMeasureGuard() {
            if (typeof ScrollTrigger === 'undefined') return;
             
            ScrollTrigger.addEventListener('refreshInit', unstickForMeasure);
            ScrollTrigger.addEventListener('refresh', restoreStickyAfterMeasure);
        }

        installStickyMeasureGuard();
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', installStickyMeasureGuard);

        









        var layoutResizeFns = [];
        var lastLayoutW = window.innerWidth;
        var lastLayoutOrient = window.innerWidth > window.innerHeight ? 'landscape' : 'portrait';

        function runLayoutResizeFns() {
            for (var i = 0; i < layoutResizeFns.length; i++) {
                try { layoutResizeFns[i](); } catch (e) {   }
            }
        }

        window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
            var w = window.innerWidth;
            var o = w > window.innerHeight ? 'landscape' : 'portrait';
             
            if (Math.abs(w - lastLayoutW) <= 1 && o === lastLayoutOrient) return;
            lastLayoutW = w;
            lastLayoutOrient = o;
            runLayoutResizeFns();
        });

        window.addEventListener('orientationchange', function () {
            lastLayoutW = window.innerWidth;
            lastLayoutOrient = lastLayoutW > window.innerHeight ? 'landscape' : 'portrait';
            runLayoutResizeFns();
        });

         
         
        function onLayoutResize(fn) {
            if (typeof fn !== 'function') return;
            if (layoutResizeFns.indexOf(fn) === -1) layoutResizeFns.push(fn);
        }

        function offLayoutResize(fn) {
            var i = layoutResizeFns.indexOf(fn);
            if (i !== -1) layoutResizeFns.splice(i, 1);
        }

        window.onLayoutResize = onLayoutResize;
        window.offLayoutResize = offLayoutResize;

        var refreshTimers = [];
        var refreshTrailingTimer = null;
        var refreshFirstPendingAt = 0;

        function refreshMainScrollTriggers() {
            if (typeof ScrollTrigger === 'undefined') return;
            ScrollTrigger.refresh();
        }

        function requestMainScrollTriggerRefresh() {
            var now = Date.now();
            if (!refreshTrailingTimer) refreshFirstPendingAt = now;
            var wait = 120;
            if (now + wait > refreshFirstPendingAt + 800) {
                wait = Math.max(0, refreshFirstPendingAt + 800 - now);
            }
            if (refreshTrailingTimer) clearTimeout(refreshTrailingTimer);
            refreshTrailingTimer = setTimeout(function () {
                refreshTrailingTimer = null;
                requestAnimationFrame(refreshMainScrollTriggers);
            }, wait);
        }

        function scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh(delays) {
            delays = delays || [0, 120, 360, 800];

             
            refreshTimers.forEach(clearTimeout);
            refreshTimers.length = 0;
            delays.forEach(function (delay) {
                refreshTimers.push(setTimeout(requestMainScrollTriggerRefresh, delay));
            });
        }

        window.refreshMainScrollTriggers = refreshMainScrollTriggers;
        window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh = scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh;

        if (document.readyState === 'complete') {
            scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh([0, 120, 360, 800, 1400]);
        } else {
            window.addEventListener('load', function () {
                scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh([0, 120, 360, 800, 1400]);
            }, { once: true });
        }

        window.addEventListener('pageshow', function () {
            scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh([0, 250]);
        });

        if (document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) {
            document.fonts.ready.then(function () {
                scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh([0, 120]);
            });
        }

         
         
        (function () {
            var pendingImgs = 0;
            document.querySelectorAll('img').forEach(function (img) {
                 
                if (img.loading === 'lazy') return;
                if (img.complete) return;
                pendingImgs++;
                var settle = function () {
                    pendingImgs--;
                    if (pendingImgs <= 0) scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh([0, 120]);
                };
                img.addEventListener('load', settle, { once: true });
                img.addEventListener('error', settle, { once: true });
            });
            if (pendingImgs > 0) {
                var correction = setInterval(function () {
                    if (pendingImgs <= 0) { clearInterval(correction); return; }
                    requestMainScrollTriggerRefresh();
                }, 1000);
                setTimeout(function () { clearInterval(correction); }, 15000);
            }
        })();
    })();

    (function () {
        function splitTitleChars(title) {
            var target = title.querySelector('p > span') || title;
            var text = target.textContent.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
            var fragment = document.createDocumentFragment();
            target.textContent = '';

            Array.prototype.forEach.call(text, function (char) {
                var span = document.createElement('span');
                span.className = 'line-inner';
                span.innerHTML = char === ' ' ? '&nbsp;' : char;
                fragment.appendChild(span);
            });

            target.appendChild(fragment);

            return target.querySelectorAll('.line-inner');
        }

        function initSectBigTitleScrollTrigger() {
            if (typeof gsap === 'undefined' || typeof ScrollTrigger === 'undefined') return;

            gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);

            window.resetSectBigTitleReveal = function (scope) {
                var root = scope || document;

                gsap.utils.toArray(root.querySelectorAll('.sect_tit_box .big_tit.is-char-reveal')).forEach(function (title) {
                    var chars = title.querySelectorAll('.line-inner');

                    if (title.sectBigTitleRevealTween) {
                        title.sectBigTitleRevealTween.pause(0);
                    }

                    if (chars.length) {
                        gsap.set(chars, {
                            yPercent: 140,
                            force3D: true
                        });
                    }
                });
            };

            gsap.utils.toArray('.sect_tit_box .big_tit').forEach(function (title) {
                if (title.sectBigTitleRevealReady) return;

                title.sectBigTitleRevealReady = true;

                var chars = splitTitleChars(title);
                if (!chars.length) return;

                if (title.closest('.character_sect')) {
                    gsap.set(chars, {
                        yPercent: 0,
                        force3D: true
                    });
                    return;
                }

                title.classList.add('is-char-reveal');

                gsap.set(chars, {
                    yPercent: 140,
                    force3D: true
                });

                title.sectBigTitleRevealTween = gsap.fromTo(
                    chars,
                    {
                        yPercent: 140
                    },
                    {
                        yPercent: 0,
                        duration: 1.2,
                        ease: 'expo.out',
                        stagger: 0.08,
                        scrollTrigger: {
                            trigger: title,
                            start: 'top 85%'
                        }
                    }
                );
            });

            if (window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh) {
                window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh([0, 120, 360]);
            } else {
                ScrollTrigger.refresh();
            }
        }

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initSectBigTitleScrollTrigger);
        } else {
            initSectBigTitleScrollTrigger();
        }
    })();

    (function () {
        function initIntroScrollTrigger() {
            if (typeof gsap === 'undefined' || typeof ScrollTrigger === 'undefined') return;

            var introSect = document.querySelector('.intro_sect');
            var introBg = introSect ? introSect.querySelector('.intro_bg') : null;
            var header = document.querySelector('.header');
            var introVideoTitleSpan = '.intro_sect .video_txt_box .tit span';
            var introVideoTitBox = introSect ? introSect.querySelector('.video_txt_box .tit_box') : null;
            var counterCont = introSect ? introSect.querySelector('.counter_cont') : null;
            var bgTit = document.querySelectorAll('.intro_sect .counter_cont .bg_txt_wrap .bg_tit');
            var counterCharacter = document.querySelector('.intro_sect .counter_cont .counter_character');
            var deskImgBox = introSect ? introSect.querySelector('.desk_img_box') : null;
            var deskImg = deskImgBox ? deskImgBox.querySelector('.desk_img') : null;
            var serviceTxtBox = document.querySelector('.intro_sect .counter_cont .service_txt_box');
            var serviceImgBox = document.querySelector('.intro_sect .counter_cont .service_txt_box .img_box');
            var serviceTxt = document.querySelector('.intro_sect .counter_cont .service_txt_box .txt');
            var orderSect = document.querySelector('.order_sect');
            var orderRobotImg = document.querySelector('.order_sect .robot_cont .img img');
            if (!introSect || !counterCont || bgTit.length < 2 || !counterCharacter || !deskImgBox || !deskImg || !serviceTxtBox || !serviceImgBox || !serviceTxt) return;

            gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);

            var bgTitEnterDuration = 0.75;
            var counterCharacterDuration = 0.4;
            var serviceTxtRevealTime = 0;
            var introBgLightTime = 0;
            var serviceTxtActive = false;
            var orderRobotFloatTween = null;


            function getCounterBottomToServiceImgBox() {
                var counterContRect = counterCharacter.parentElement.getBoundingClientRect();
                var imgBoxRect = serviceImgBox.getBoundingClientRect();
                var serviceY = gsap.getProperty(serviceTxtBox, 'y') || 0;
                var imgBoxBottomAtYZero = imgBoxRect.bottom - serviceY;

                return counterContRect.bottom - imgBoxBottomAtYZero;
            }

            function getDeskImgBottomToServiceImgBox() {
                var deskImgBoxRect = deskImgBox.getBoundingClientRect();
                var imgBoxRect = serviceImgBox.getBoundingClientRect();
                var serviceY = gsap.getProperty(serviceTxtBox, 'y') || 0;
                var imgBoxBottomAtYZero = imgBoxRect.bottom - serviceY;

                return deskImgBoxRect.bottom * 1 - imgBoxBottomAtYZero;
            }

            function wrapServiceTxtLetters() {
                var text = serviceTxt.textContent.trim();
                var fragment = document.createDocumentFragment();

                serviceTxt.textContent = '';
                Array.from(text).forEach(function (letter) {
                    var span = document.createElement('span');
                    span.className = 'char';
                    span.textContent = letter === ' ' ? '\u00a0' : letter;
                    fragment.appendChild(span);
                });
                serviceTxt.appendChild(fragment);

                return serviceTxt.querySelectorAll('.char');
            }

            function showServiceTxt() {
                if (serviceTxtActive) return;

                serviceTxtActive = true;
                serviceTxt.classList.add('active');
                gsap.killTweensOf(serviceTxtLetters);
                gsap.set(serviceTxtLetters, {
                    autoAlpha: 0
                });
                gsap.to(serviceTxtLetters, {
                    autoAlpha: 1,
                    duration: 0.01,
                    stagger: 0.045,
                    ease: 'none',
                    overwrite: true
                });
            }

            function hideServiceTxt() {
                if (!serviceTxtActive) return;

                serviceTxtActive = false;
                serviceTxt.classList.remove('active');
                gsap.killTweensOf(serviceTxtLetters);
                gsap.set(serviceTxtLetters, {
                    autoAlpha: 0,
                    overwrite: true
                });
            }

            function startOrderRobotFloat() {
                if (!orderRobotImg) return;
                if (orderRobotFloatTween) return;

                orderRobotFloatTween = gsap.to(orderRobotImg, {
                    y: -8,
                    rotation: -0.25,
                    duration: 1.8,
                    ease: 'sine.inOut',
                    yoyo: true,
                    repeat: -1,
                    transformOrigin: '50% 100%',
                    keyframes: [
                        { y: -8, rotation: -0.25, duration: 1.8, ease: 'sine.inOut' },
                        { y: -3, rotation: 0.2, duration: 1.8, ease: 'sine.inOut' }
                    ]
                });
            }

            function stopOrderRobotFloat() {
                if (!orderRobotImg) return;
                if (orderRobotFloatTween) {
                    orderRobotFloatTween.kill();
                    orderRobotFloatTween = null;
                }

                gsap.set(orderRobotImg, {
                    x: 0,
                    y: 0,
                    rotation: 0,
                    force3D: true
                });
            }

            function updateIntroBgLight(time) {
                if (!introBgLightTime) return;

                var introBgLightDelay = 0.15;
                var triggerTime = introBgLightTime + introBgLightDelay;

                var orderSectAdvance = 0.3;
                var orderTriggerTime = triggerTime - orderSectAdvance;
                var isOrderSectOn = time >= orderTriggerTime;

                orderSect.classList.toggle('on', isOrderSectOn);

                if (isOrderSectOn) startOrderRobotFloat();
                else stopOrderRobotFloat();

                introSect.classList.toggle('is-bg-light', time >= triggerTime);
                orderSect.classList.toggle('is-bg-light', time >= triggerTime);

                if (header) header.classList.toggle('color_type', time >= triggerTime);
            }

            var serviceTxtLetters = wrapServiceTxtLetters();

            gsap.set(counterCharacter, {
                xPercent: -50,
                yPercent: 100,
                x: 0,
                y: 0
            });
            gsap.set(deskImgBox, {
                y: 0,
                autoAlpha: 1
            });
            gsap.set(deskImg, {
                xPercent: -50,
                yPercent: 42,
                y: '100svh'
            });
            gsap.set(serviceTxtLetters, {
                autoAlpha: 0
            });

            var introTimeline = gsap.timeline({
                scrollTrigger: {
                    trigger: introSect,
                    start: 'top top',
                    endTrigger: introSect,
                    end: 'bottom bottom',
                    scrub: true,
                    markers: false,
                    invalidateOnRefresh: true,
                    onUpdate: function (self) {
                        if (self.animation && serviceTxtRevealTime && self.animation.time() < serviceTxtRevealTime) {
                            hideServiceTxt();
                        }

                        if (self.animation) {
                            updateIntroBgLight(self.animation.time());
                        }
                    }
                }
            })
                .set(introVideoTitleSpan, {
                    transition: 'none',
                    transitionDelay: '0s'
                })
                .set(".intro_sect .video_txt_box span", {
                    transition: 'none',
                    transitionDelay: '0s'
                })
                .to({}, {
                    duration: 0.2,
                    onStart: function () {
                        if (introVideoTitBox) introVideoTitBox.classList.add('hide');
                    },
                    onReverseComplete: function () {
                        if (introVideoTitBox) introVideoTitBox.classList.remove('hide');
                    }
                })
                .to('.intro_sect .intro_bg', {
                    scale: 1,
                    duration: 0.9,
                    ease: 'none',
                }, "<")
                .to('.intro_sect .intro_bg', {
                    opacity: 0,
                    duration: 0.3,
                }, ">-0.2")
                .to('.intro_sect .intro_bg .item', {
                    filter: 'blur(10px)',
                    duration: 1,
                }, "<")
                .to('.dealy', {
                    duration: 0.3,
                }, ">")
                .addLabel('exit', '+=0.25')
                .to(deskImg, {
                    y: 0,
                    xPercent: -50,
                    yPercent: 42,
                    ease: 'none'
                }, 'exit-=0.3')
                .to(counterCharacter, {
                    xPercent: -50,
                    yPercent: 0,
                    x: 0,
                    y: 0,
                    duration: counterCharacterDuration,
                    ease: 'none'
                }, 'exit')
                .fromTo(bgTit, {
                    '--bg-tit-x': function (index) {
                        return index === 0 ? '100vw' : '-100vw';
                    }
                }, {
                    '--bg-tit-x': function (index) {
                        return index === 0 ? '-2vw' : '49vw';
                    },
                    duration: bgTitEnterDuration,
                    ease: 'none'
                }, 'exit-=0.4')
                .addLabel('counterToService', 'exit+=0.85')
                .to(".bg_txt_wrap", {
                    transform: 'translate(0,-100%)',
                    top: 0,
                    ease: 'none'
                }, 'counterToService')
                .to(counterCharacter, {
                    width: "61rem",
                    bottom: function () {
                        return getCounterBottomToServiceImgBox();
                    },
                    left: "50.6%",
                    xPercent: -50,
                    yPercent: 0,
                    x: 0,
                    y: 0,
                    duration: counterCharacterDuration,
                    ease: 'none'
                }, 'counterToService+=0.2')
                .to(deskImg, {
                    bottom: function () {
                        return getDeskImgBottomToServiceImgBox();
                    },
                    yPercent: 45,
                    width: window.innerWidth <= 768 ? '26rem' : '28rem',
                    ease: 'none'
                }, 'counterToService+=0.2')
                .to(serviceTxtBox, {
                    y: 0,
                    ease: 'none',
                }, 'counterToService+=0.2')
                .to(deskImg, {
                    clipPath: window.innerWidth <= 600 ? "inset(0px 0px 45% round 0px 0px 0% 0%)" : "inset(0px 0px 45% round  0 0 0 0%)",
                    duration: window.innerWidth <= 600 ? '0.045' : '0.065',
                    ease: 'none'
                }, window.innerWidth <= 600 ? 'counterToService+=0.655' : 'counterToService+=0.635')
                .to(serviceImgBox, {
                    width: window.innerWidth <= 600 ? "35rem" : (window.innerWidth <= 768 ? "40rem" : "41rem"),
                    opacity: 1,
                    ease: 'none'
                }, 'counterToService')
                .to("delay", {
                    duration: "0.4"
                })
                .addLabel('serviceTxtReveal', 'counterToService+=0.35')
                .call(function () {
                    showServiceTxt();
                }, null, 'serviceTxtReveal')
                .to("delay", {
                    duration: "0.25"
                })
                .to(counterCont, {
                    y: '-100%',
                    ease: "none"
                }, ">-0.45")
                .to(deskImgBox, {
                    y: '-100%',
                    ease: "none"
                }, "<")
                .addLabel('introBgLight', ">-0.3")
                .to(counterCharacter, {
                    opacity: 0,
                    duration: 0.25,
                    ease: "none"
                }, 'introBgLight')
                .to(deskImgBox, {
                    opacity: 0,
                    duration: 0.25,
                    ease: "none"
                }, 'introBgLight')
                .to(serviceTxtBox, {
                    opacity: 0,
                    duration: 0.25,
                    ease: "none"
                }, 'introBgLight');

            serviceTxtRevealTime = introTimeline.labels.serviceTxtReveal;
            introBgLightTime = introTimeline.labels.introBgLight;

             
             
             
            function refreshIntroScrollTrigger() {
                if (window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh) {
                    window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh([0, 120, 360]);
                } else {
                    ScrollTrigger.refresh();
                }
            }

            if (document.readyState === 'complete') {
                refreshIntroScrollTrigger();
            } else {
                window.addEventListener('load', refreshIntroScrollTrigger, { once: true });
            }

            if (document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) {
                document.fonts.ready.then(refreshIntroScrollTrigger);
            }
        }

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initIntroScrollTrigger);
        } else {
            initIntroScrollTrigger();
        }
    })();

    (function () {
        function cloneBandMoveText() {
            document.querySelectorAll('.band_slide_cont .moveText .text').forEach(function (text) {
                var originalItems = Array.prototype.slice.call(text.children);
                if (!originalItems.length) return;

                while ((text.children.length < 12 || text.scrollWidth < window.innerWidth * 4) && text.children.length < 36) {
                    originalItems.forEach(function (item) {
                        var clone = item.cloneNode(true);
                        clone.classList.add('is-clone');
                        clone.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
                        text.appendChild(clone);
                    });
                }
            });
        }

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', cloneBandMoveText);
        } else {
            cloneBandMoveText();
        }
    })();

    (function () {
        var lastScrollY = window.pageYOffset || document.documentElement.scrollTop || 0;
        var moveTextRows = [];
        var scrollVelocity = 0;
        var scrollDirection = 1;

         
         
         
        var bandSlideContEl = null;
        var bandVisible = false;
        var bandObserver = null;

        function observeBandVisibility() {
            if (!bandSlideContEl || typeof IntersectionObserver === 'undefined') {
                bandVisible = true;
                return;
            }
            if (bandObserver) bandObserver.disconnect();
            bandObserver = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
                bandVisible = entries[entries.length - 1].isIntersecting;
            });
            bandObserver.observe(bandSlideContEl);
        }

        function collectBandRows() {
            var bandSlideCont = bandSlideContEl = document.querySelector('.order_now_sect .band_slide_cont');
            observeBandVisibility();
            if (!bandSlideCont) return;

            moveTextRows = [];

            bandSlideCont.querySelectorAll('.moveText .text').forEach(function (text) {
                var firstItem = text.children[0];
                var secondItem = text.children[1];
                if (!firstItem) return;

                moveTextRows.push({
                    item: text,
                    width: secondItem ? secondItem.offsetLeft - firstItem.offsetLeft : firstItem.offsetWidth || 1,
                    direction: -1,
                    x: 0
                });
            });
        }

        function renderMoveTextRow(row) {
            var width = row.width || 1;
            var x = ((-row.x % width) + width) % width;

            row.item.style.transform = 'translate3d(' + (-x) + 'px, 0, 0)';
        }

        function updateBandScrollSpeed(delta) {
            if (!delta) return;

            scrollDirection = delta < 0 ? -1 : 1;
            scrollVelocity += Math.abs(Math.max(-220, Math.min(220, delta))) * 12;
        }

        function updateBandScrollSpeedByPageScroll() {
            var scrollY = window.pageYOffset || document.documentElement.scrollTop || 0;
            var delta = scrollY - lastScrollY;
            lastScrollY = scrollY;
            updateBandScrollSpeed(delta);
        }

        function tickBandFlow() {
             
            if (!bandSlideContEl || !bandVisible || !bandSlideContEl.classList.contains('active')) {
                scrollVelocity *= 0.82;
                return;
            }

            var deltaRatio = typeof gsap !== 'undefined' && gsap.ticker ? gsap.ticker.deltaRatio(60) : 1;
            var seconds = deltaRatio / 60;
            var baseSpeed = 60;

            moveTextRows.forEach(function (row) {
                var speedDirection = scrollVelocity > 1 ? scrollDirection : 1;
                row.x += (baseSpeed * 0.45 + scrollVelocity * 0.35) * row.direction * speedDirection * seconds;
                renderMoveTextRow(row);
            });

            scrollVelocity *= 0.82;

            if (scrollVelocity < 1) {
                scrollVelocity = 0;
                scrollDirection = 1;
            }
        }

        



        






        (function initOffscreenVideoGate() {
            var vids = document.querySelectorAll('video[data-autoplay]:not([data-timeline-play])');
            if (!vids.length) return;

            var play = function (v) {
                if (!v.paused) return;
                var p = v.play();
                if (p && p.catch) p.catch(function () {   });
            };

            if (!('IntersectionObserver' in window)) {
                vids.forEach(play);    
                return;
            }

            



            var inView = new WeakSet();

            var io = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
                entries.forEach(function (e) {
                    if (e.isIntersecting) {
                        inView.add(e.target);
                        if (!document.hidden) play(e.target);
                    } else {
                        inView.delete(e.target);
                        if (!e.target.paused) e.target.pause();
                    }
                });
            }, { rootMargin: '150% 0px' });

            vids.forEach(function (v) { io.observe(v); });

            


            document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () {
                vids.forEach(function (v) {
                    if (document.hidden) {
                        if (!v.paused) v.pause();
                    } else if (inView.has(v)) {
                        play(v);
                    }
                });
            });
        })();

        function initBandScrollSpeed() {
            if (typeof gsap === 'undefined') return;

            collectBandRows();
            window.addEventListener('scroll', updateBandScrollSpeedByPageScroll, { passive: true });
            window.addEventListener('resize', collectBandRows);
            window.addEventListener('load', collectBandRows, { once: true });
            gsap.ticker.add(tickBandFlow);
        }

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initBandScrollSpeed);
        } else {
            initBandScrollSpeed();
        }
    })();

    (function () {
        function initOrderScrollTrigger() {
            if (typeof gsap === 'undefined' || typeof ScrollTrigger === 'undefined') return;

            var orderIconCont = document.querySelector('.order_sect .icon_cont');
            var orderItems = gsap.utils.toArray('.order_sect .cont');
            if (!orderIconCont || !orderItems.length) return;

            gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);


            gsap.set(orderItems, {
                y: 0,
                opacity: 1
            });

            var orderMoveDelay = 0.3;
            var orderMoveItems = [
                { selector: '.order_sect .cont.num1', start: 0, duration: 0.7, y: '-208svh', ease: 'sine.inOut' },
                { selector: '.order_sect .cont.num3', start: 0.08, duration: 0.9, y: '-218svh', ease: 'power1.inOut' },
                { selector: '.order_sect .cont.num2', start: 0.1, duration: 0.7, y: '-242svh', ease: 'power1.in' },
                { selector: '.order_sect .cont.num4', start: 0.3, duration: 0.9, y: '-238svh', ease: 'power1.inOut' },
                { selector: '.order_sect .cont.num5', start: 0.2, duration: 0.8, y: '-285svh', ease: 'power2.in' },

                { selector: '.order_sect .cont.num6', start: 0.1, duration: 0.8, y: '-242svh', ease: 'sine.inOut' },

                { selector: '.order_sect .cont.num7', start: 0.3, duration: 1.05, y: '-306svh', ease: 'power1.in' },
                { selector: '.order_sect .cont.num8', start: 0.1, duration: 0.75, y: '-262svh', ease: 'power2.in' },

                { selector: '.order_sect .robot_cont', start: 0.2, duration: 0.7, y: '-150svh', ease: 'power2.in' }
            ];
            var orderTimeline = gsap.timeline({
                scrollTrigger: {
                    trigger: orderIconCont,
                    start: 'top center',
                    end: 'bottom bottom',
                    scrub: true,
                    // markers: true,
                    invalidateOnRefresh: true
                }
            });
            orderMoveItems.forEach(function (item) {
                var target = document.querySelector(item.selector);

                if (target) {
                    ScrollTrigger.create({
                        trigger: target,
                        start: 'center bottom',
                        










                        refreshPriority: 1,
                        onEnter: function () {
                            target.classList.add('active');
                        },
                        onLeaveBack: function () {
                            target.classList.remove('active');
                        }
                    });
                }

                orderTimeline.to(item.selector, {
                    y: item.y,
                    duration: item.duration,
                    ease: item.ease
                }, orderMoveDelay + item.start);
            });

        }

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initOrderScrollTrigger);
        } else {
            initOrderScrollTrigger();
        }
    })();

            function getMainInnerPadding() {
                var inPdValue = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--mainInPd').trim();
                if (!inPdValue) return 0;

                var measureEl = document.createElement('div');
                measureEl.style.position = 'absolute';
                measureEl.style.visibility = 'hidden';
                measureEl.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
                measureEl.style.width = inPdValue;
                document.body.appendChild(measureEl);

                var width = measureEl.getBoundingClientRect().width;
                document.body.removeChild(measureEl);

                return width;
            }

            function getHeaderHeightOffset() {
                var hdHValue = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--hdH').trim();
                if (!hdHValue) return 0;

                var measureEl = document.createElement('div');
                measureEl.style.position = 'absolute';
                measureEl.style.visibility = 'hidden';
                measureEl.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
                measureEl.style.height = hdHValue;
                document.body.appendChild(measureEl);

                var height = measureEl.getBoundingClientRect().height;
                document.body.removeChild(measureEl);

                return height;
            }

                var moveCenterX = moveRect.left + (moveRect.width * 0.5);

            window.recalibratePeopleMoveLanding = function (attempt) {
                var tl = peopleCharacterTimeline;
                if (!tl || !tl.labels) return;

                var startAt = tl.labels.peopleMoveStart;
                if (typeof startAt !== 'number') return;

                var mv = tl.getById('peopleMoveX');
                if (!mv) return;

                var now = tl.time();
                 
                if (now < startAt) return;

                



                var tries = typeof attempt === 'number' ? attempt : 0;
                var recheck = function () {
                    if (tries >= 3) return;
                    setTimeout(function () {
                        if (Math.abs(getPeopleMoveTargetX()) > 8) {
                            window.recalibratePeopleMoveLanding(tries + 1);
                        }
                    }, 400);
                };

                 
                 
                if (now >= startAt + mv.duration() && Math.abs(getPeopleMoveTargetX()) <= 8) {
                    









                    recheck();
                    return;
                }

                










                peopleSect.classList.add('is-remeasuring');
                tl.time(startAt, true);
                mv.invalidate();
                tl.time(now, true);
                peopleSect.classList.remove('is-remeasuring');

                recheck();
            };

            if (window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh) {
                window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh([0, 120, 360]);
            } else {
                ScrollTrigger.refresh();
            }
        }

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initPeopleBasicCharacterScrollTrigger);
        } else {
            initPeopleBasicCharacterScrollTrigger();
        }
    })();

    (function () {
        function initCharacterScrollTrigger() {
            if (typeof gsap === 'undefined' || typeof ScrollTrigger === 'undefined') return;
            if (window.innerWidth <= 1040) return;

            var characterWrap = document.querySelector('.portfolio_sect');
            var portfolioWrap = document.querySelector('.portfolio_sect .portfolio_wrap');
            if (!characterWrap || !portfolioWrap) return;

            gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);

            portfolioWrap.classList.remove('is-character-scroll-ready');

            var characterTimeline = gsap.timeline({
                scrollTrigger: {
                    trigger: characterWrap,
                    start: function () {
                        return 'top bottom';
                    },
                    end: function () {
                        return 'top top';
                    },
                    markers: false,
                    scrub: true,
                    refreshPriority: 2,
                    invalidateOnRefresh: true,
                    onRefreshInit: function () {
                        portfolioWrap.classList.remove('is-character-scroll-ready');
                    },
                    onRefresh: function () {
                        portfolioWrap.classList.add('is-character-scroll-ready');

                    }
                }
            }).to(portfolioWrap, {
                x: 0,
                y: 0,
                xPercent: 0,
                yPercent: 0,
                rotation: 0,
                scale: 1,
                ease: 'none',
                immediateRender: false
            });

            setTimeout(function () {
                portfolioWrap.classList.add('is-character-scroll-ready');
            }, 1000)

            window.characterScrollTrigger = characterTimeline.scrollTrigger;

            if (window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh) {
                window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh([0, 120, 360, 800, 1400, 2200]);
            }

            window.addEventListener('load', function () {
                if (window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh) {
                    window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh([0, 120, 360, 800]);
                } else {
                    ScrollTrigger.refresh();
                }
            }, { once: true });

        }

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initCharacterScrollTrigger);
        } else {
            initCharacterScrollTrigger();
        }
    })();

                })

                .to('.beyond_sect .gra_bg_wrap .gradient.w78', { y: "-110vh", duration: 0.2, ease: "none" }, "<+0.045")
                .to('.beyond_sect .gra_bg_wrap .gradient.w52', { y: "-110vh", duration: 0.15, ease: "none" }, "<+0.045")
                .to('.beyond_sect .gra_bg_wrap .gradient.w26', {
                    y: "-110vh", duration: 0.1, ease: "none",
                }, "<+0.045")
                .to('.beyond_sect .gra_bg_wrap .gra_bg_cont .bg_color', {
                    opacity: 1,
                    duration: 0.05,
                }, ">")






            var beyondGraBgActive = gsap.timeline({
                scrollTrigger: {
                    trigger: ".beyond_sect .cont_wrap",
                    start: 'bottom 25%',
                    end: 'bottom',
                    scrub: true,
                    invalidateOnRefresh: true,
                    onEnter: () => {
                        $('.beyond_sect .gra_bg_wrap').addClass('wh');
                    },
                    onLeaveBack: () => {
                        $('.beyond_sect .gra_bg_wrap').removeClass('wh');
                    },
                }
            });







        }

        function initBeyondReviewScrollTrigger() {
            if (typeof gsap === 'undefined' || typeof ScrollTrigger === 'undefined') return;

            var reviewConts = document.querySelectorAll('.beyond_sect .review_cont .cont');
            if (!reviewConts.length) return;

            gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);

            var reviewTimelines = [];

            reviewConts.forEach(function (cont) {
                var txtWrap = cont.querySelector('.txt_wrap');
                var isLastCont = cont === reviewConts[reviewConts.length - 1];
                if (!txtWrap) return;

                var targets = [
                    { el: txtWrap.querySelector('.star'), opacity: 1 },
                    { el: txtWrap.querySelector('.tit'), opacity: 1 },
                    { el: txtWrap.querySelector('.txt'), opacity: 0.6 },
                    { el: txtWrap.querySelector('.name_txt'), opacity: 1 },
                    { el: txtWrap.querySelector('.tag'), opacity: 1 }
                ].filter(function (item) {
                    return item.el;
                });

                if (!targets.length) return;

                gsap.set(txtWrap, { opacity: 1 });

                var onTimeline = gsap.timeline({
                    scrollTrigger: {
                        trigger: cont,
                        start: 'top center+=25%',
                        end: 'center center',
                        scrub: true,
                        markers: false,
                        invalidateOnRefresh: true
                    }
                });

                targets.forEach(function (item, index) {
                    onTimeline.to(item.el, {
                        opacity: item.opacity,
                        duration: 0.2,
                        ease: 'none'
                    }, index * 0.1);
                });

                if (isLastCont) {
                    onTimeline.to(cont, {
                        borderBottomColor: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 1)',
                        duration: 0.2,
                        ease: 'none'
                    }, 0.2);
                }

                var offTimeline = gsap.timeline({
                    scrollTrigger: {
                        trigger: cont,
                        start: 'bottom center',
                        end: 'top top',
                        scrub: true,
                        markers: false,
                        invalidateOnRefresh: true
                    }
                });

                targets.forEach(function (item) {
                    offTimeline.to(item.el, {
                        opacity: 0.1,
                        duration: 0.5,
                        ease: 'none'
                    }, 0);
                });

                if (isLastCont) {
                    offTimeline.to(cont, {
                        borderBottomColor: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2)',
                        duration: 0.5,
                        ease: 'none'
                    }, 0);
                }

                reviewTimelines.push(onTimeline, offTimeline);
            });

            window.beyondReviewTimelines = reviewTimelines;
        }

        function initAwardSection() {
            initAwardScrollTrigger();
            initBeyondGraBgScrollTrigger();
            initBeyondReviewScrollTrigger();
        }

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initAwardSection);
        } else {
            initAwardSection();
        }
    })();

    (function () {
        function initPortfolioScrollTrigger() {
            if (typeof gsap === 'undefined' || typeof ScrollTrigger === 'undefined') return;
            if (window.innerWidth <= 1040) return;

            gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);

            var portfolioAnimations = [];

            function updatePortfolioAnimations() {
                 
                 
                var viewportHeight = window.innerHeight;
                var startTop = viewportHeight * 1.1;

                portfolioAnimations.forEach(function (portfolioAnimation) {
                    var contBoxRect = portfolioAnimation.contBox.getBoundingClientRect();
                    var endTop = (viewportHeight / 2) - (contBoxRect.height / 2);
                    var scrollDistance = startTop - endTop;
                    var progress = scrollDistance > 0
                        ? (startTop - contBoxRect.top) / scrollDistance
                        : 0;

                    portfolioAnimation.timeline.progress(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, progress)));
                });
            }

            gsap.utils.toArray('.portfolio_sect .portfolio_wrap .portfolio_cont .cont_box').forEach(function (contBox) {
                var items = gsap.utils.toArray(contBox.querySelectorAll('.cont'));
                if (!items.length) return;

                var firstGroup = [items[0], items[3]].filter(Boolean);
                var secondGroup = [items[1], items[2]].filter(Boolean);
                var firstImgs = firstGroup.map(function (item) { return item.querySelector('.img'); }).filter(Boolean);
                var secondImgs = secondGroup.map(function (item) { return item.querySelector('.img'); }).filter(Boolean);
                var firstHoverBoxes = firstGroup.map(function (item) { return item.querySelector('.hover_box'); }).filter(Boolean);
                var secondHoverBoxes = secondGroup.map(function (item) { return item.querySelector('.hover_box'); }).filter(Boolean);
                var firstTxts = firstGroup.map(function (item) { return item.querySelector('.txt_box'); }).filter(Boolean);
                var secondTxts = secondGroup.map(function (item) { return item.querySelector('.txt_box'); }).filter(Boolean);

                gsap.set(items.map(function (item) { return item.querySelector('.img'); }).filter(Boolean), {
                    transformOrigin: 'center bottom',
                    transformPerspective: 1200
                });
                gsap.set(items.map(function (item) { return item.querySelector('.hover_box'); }).filter(Boolean), {
                    y: '8vw',
                    rotateX: 110,
                    scale: 1.1,
                    transformOrigin: 'center bottom',
                    transformPerspective: 1200
                });

                var portfolioTimeline = gsap.timeline({ paused: true });

                portfolioTimeline
                    .to(firstImgs.concat(firstHoverBoxes), {
                        y: 0,
                        rotateX: 0,
                        scale: 1,
                        duration: 0.72,
                        ease: 'power2.out'
                    }, 0)
                    .to(firstTxts, {
                        opacity: 1,
                        y: 0,
                        duration: 0.48,
                        ease: 'power1.out'
                    }, 0.18)
                    .to(secondImgs.concat(secondHoverBoxes), {
                        y: 0,
                        rotateX: 0,
                        scale: 1,
                        duration: 0.76,
                        ease: 'power3.out'
                    }, 0.18)
                    .to(secondTxts, {
                        opacity: 1,
                        y: 0,
                        duration: 0.48,
                        ease: 'power1.out'
                    }, 0.36);

                portfolioAnimations.push({
                    contBox: contBox,
                    timeline: portfolioTimeline
                });
            });

             
             
             
            ScrollTrigger.create({
                trigger: '.portfolio_sect',
                start: 'top bottom',
                end: 'bottom top',
                refreshPriority: -2,
                onUpdate: updatePortfolioAnimations
            });

            ScrollTrigger.addEventListener('refresh', updatePortfolioAnimations);
            updatePortfolioAnimations();

            if (window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh) {
                window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh([0, 120, 360, 800, 1400, 2200]);
            }

            window.addEventListener('load', function () {
                if (window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh) {
                    window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh([0, 120, 360, 800]);
                } else {
                    ScrollTrigger.refresh();
                }
            }, { once: true });
        }

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initPortfolioScrollTrigger);
        } else {
            initPortfolioScrollTrigger();
        }
    })();

    (function () {
        function refreshOrderNowScrollTriggers() {
            if (window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh) {
                window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh([0, 120, 360, 800, 1400, 2200]);
            } else if (typeof ScrollTrigger !== 'undefined') {
                requestAnimationFrame(function () {
                    ScrollTrigger.refresh();
                });
            }
        }

        function initOrderNowSwiper() {
            if (typeof Swiper === 'undefined') return;

            var orderNowSwiperEl = document.querySelector('.order_now_sect .order-now-swiper');
            if (!orderNowSwiperEl || orderNowSwiperEl.swiper) return;

            window.orderNowSwiper = new Swiper(orderNowSwiperEl, {
                slidesPerView: 'auto',
                spaceBetween: 30,
                breakpoints: {
                    2240: {
                        spaceBetween: 30
                    },
                    1640: {
                        spaceBetween: 20
                    },
                    600: {
                        spaceBetween: 15
                    },
                    0: {
                        spaceBetween: 10
                    },
                }

            });

            refreshOrderNowScrollTriggers();
        }

        function initOrderNowHoverMotion() {
            if (typeof gsap === 'undefined') return;

            var hoverMap = {
                left_top: { x: 15, y: 15 },
                center_top: { x: 0, y: 15 },
                right_top: { x: -15, y: 15 },
                left: { x: 15, y: 0 },
                center: { x: 0, y: 0 },
                right: { x: -15, y: 0 },
                left_bot: { x: 15, y: -15 },
                center_bot: { x: 0, y: -15 },
                right_bot: { x: -15, y: -15 }
            };

            document.querySelectorAll('.order_now_sect .slide .swiper-slide .hover_box').forEach(function (hoverBox) {
                var slide = hoverBox.closest('.swiper-slide');

                hoverBox.addEventListener('mouseleave', function () {
                    if (slide) slide.orderNowHoverLocked = false;
                });
            });

            document.querySelectorAll('.order_now_sect .slide .swiper-slide .hover_box > div').forEach(function (area) {
                area.addEventListener('mouseenter', function () {
                    var slide = area.closest('.swiper-slide');
                    var orderNowRoot = area.closest('.order_now_sect');
                    var bg = slide ? slide.querySelector('.bg') : null;
                    if (!bg || slide.orderNowHoverLocked || (orderNowRoot && orderNowRoot.classList.contains('is-order-compacting'))) return;

                    slide.orderNowHoverLocked = true;

                    var motion = hoverMap[area.className] || { x: 0, y: 0 };
                    gsap.killTweensOf(bg);
                    gsap.fromTo(bg,
                        { x: 0, y: 0 },
                        {
                            x: motion.x,
                            y: motion.y,
                            duration: 0.25,
                            ease: 'cubic-bezier(0.25, 0.1, 0.25, 1)',
                            yoyo: true,
                            repeat: 1,
                            overwrite: true
                        }
                    );
                });
            });
        }

        function initOrderNowScrollTrigger() {
            if (typeof gsap === 'undefined' || typeof ScrollTrigger === 'undefined') return;

            var orderNowTrigger = document.querySelector('.order_now_sect .sticky_box');
            var orderNowSticky = document.querySelector('.order_now_sect .sticky');
            var orderNowSlide = document.querySelector('.order_now_sect .slide');
            var orderNowRoot = document.querySelector('.order_now_sect');
            var orderNowSwiperEl = document.querySelector('.order_now_sect .order-now-swiper');
            var bandSlideCont = document.querySelector('.order_now_sect .band_slide_cont');
            var orderNowSince2TitleChars = [];
            if (!orderNowTrigger || !orderNowSticky || !orderNowSlide || !orderNowRoot) return;
            var enableOrderNowCompactTimeline = window.innerWidth > 1040;




            gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);

            var orderNowCardDelay = [0, 0.7, 0.35, 0.2, 0.4, 0.8, 0.6, 0.9];

            function splitOrderNowSince2TitleChars() {
                var chars = [];
                var charIndex = 0;

                gsap.utils.toArray('.order_now_sect .since2_box .main_in .tit p').forEach(function (line) {
                    var source = line.querySelector('span') || line;

                    if (!line.orderNowSplitReady) {
                        var text = source.textContent.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
                        var fragment = document.createDocumentFragment();

                        source.textContent = '';

                        Array.prototype.forEach.call(text, function (char) {
                            var span = document.createElement('span');
                            var randomSeed = (charIndex * 7 + 3) % 11;
                            span.className = 'line-inner scatter_char';
                            span.style.setProperty('--char-random', randomSeed);
                            span.style.setProperty('--i', charIndex);
                            span.innerHTML = char === ' ' ? '&nbsp;' : char;
                            fragment.appendChild(span);
                            charIndex++;
                        });

                        source.appendChild(fragment);
                        line.orderNowSplitReady = true;
                    } else {
                        gsap.utils.toArray(source.querySelectorAll('.line-inner')).forEach(function (charEl) {
                            if (!charEl.style.getPropertyValue('--i')) {
                                var randomSeed = (charIndex * 7 + 3) % 11;
                                charEl.classList.add('scatter_char');
                                charEl.style.setProperty('--char-random', randomSeed);
                                charEl.style.setProperty('--i', charIndex);
                            }
                            charIndex++;
                        });
                    }

                    chars = chars.concat(gsap.utils.toArray(source.querySelectorAll('.line-inner')));
                });

                return chars;
            }

            orderNowSince2TitleChars = splitOrderNowSince2TitleChars();

            if (orderNowSince2TitleChars.length) {
                gsap.set(orderNowSince2TitleChars, {
                    yPercent: 30,
                    opacity: 0,
                    filter: 'blur(10px)',
                    force3D: true
                });
            }

            function getOrderNowCardDelay(index) {
                if (typeof orderNowCardDelay[index] !== 'undefined') {
                    return orderNowCardDelay[index];
                }

                return index * 0.15;
            }

            function getOrderNowCardCompactDuration() {
                var maxDelay = 0;
                for (var i = 0; i < orderNowCards.length; i++) {
                    maxDelay = Math.max(maxDelay, getOrderNowCardDelay(i));
                }

                return maxDelay + 1;
            }

            function refreshCharacterTrigger() {
                requestAnimationFrame(function () {
                    if (window.characterScrollTrigger && typeof window.characterScrollTrigger.refresh === 'function') {
                        window.characterScrollTrigger.refresh();
                    }
                });
            }

            function setOrderNowCompactComplete(complete) {
                if (!orderNowRoot) return;

                if (orderNowRoot.classList.contains('is-compact-complete') === complete) return;

                orderNowRoot.classList.toggle('is-compact-complete', complete);
                refreshCharacterTrigger();
            }

            function getOrderNowIcoHeight() {
                var icoHeight = 0;

                orderNowCards.forEach(function (card) {
                    var ico = card.querySelector('.ico');
                    if (!ico) return;
                    icoHeight = Math.max(icoHeight, ico.offsetHeight);
                });

                return icoHeight;
            }

            function getOrderNowBgClipInsetHeight() {
                var clipInsetHeight = 0;

                orderNowCards.forEach(function (card) {
                    if (!card) return;
                    clipInsetHeight = Math.max(clipInsetHeight, card.offsetHeight - orderNowCompactPx);
                });

                return clipInsetHeight;
            }

            function getOrderNowSlideMoveY() {
                if (!orderNowSlide || !orderNowKeword || !orderNowSticky) return 0;

                 
                 
                 
                 
                 
                 
                 
                 
                 
                 
                var orderNowStickyStyle = window.getComputedStyle(orderNowSticky);
                var orderNowStickyTop = orderNowStickyStyle.position === 'sticky'
                    ? (parseFloat(orderNowStickyStyle.top) || 0)
                    : orderNowSticky.getBoundingClientRect().top;
                var groupTop = orderNowStickyTop + Math.min(orderNowSlide.offsetTop, orderNowKeword.offsetTop);
                var groupBottom = orderNowStickyTop + Math.max(
                    orderNowSlide.offsetTop + orderNowSlide.offsetHeight,
                    orderNowKeword.offsetTop + orderNowKeword.offsetHeight
                );
                var groupCenter = (groupTop + groupBottom) / 2;
                var viewportCenter = window.innerHeight / 2;

                return viewportCenter - groupCenter + getOrderNowIcoHeight();
            }

            function getOrderNowKewordMoveY() {
                return getOrderNowSlideMoveY() - getOrderNowBgClipInsetHeight();
            }

            // function setOrderNowSectOpacity(value) {
            //     gsap.set(orderNowRoot, {
            //         transition: 'opacity 0.1s ease',
            //         opacity: value
            //     });
