            //     gsap.set('.made_sect', {
            //         opacity: 1 - value
            //     });
            // }

            var bandHighlightLayer = document.querySelector('.order_now_sect .band_slide_cont .band_highlight_sticky');
            var bandHighlightStickyVideo = bandHighlightLayer ? bandHighlightLayer.querySelector('video') : null;
            var bandHighlightSourceVideo = document.querySelector('.order_now_sect .band_slide_cont .band.type1 .cont .img.highlight video');
            var bandHighlightTarget = document.querySelector('.order_now_sect .band_slide_cont .band.type1 .cont .img.highlight');
            var bandHighlightTargetBand = document.querySelector('.order_now_sect .band_slide_cont .band.type1');
            var bandHighlightVideos = bandHighlightLayer ? Array.prototype.slice.call(bandHighlightLayer.querySelectorAll('video')) : [];

            function getBandHighlightVideos() {
                return bandHighlightVideos;
            }

            var bandHighlightVideoCenterState = {};
            var bandHighlightVideoMotionState = { x: 0, y: 0, width: 0, height: 0, rotation: 0 };
            var bandHighlightVideoIdleState = { y: 0, rotation: 0, scale: 1 };
            var bandHighlightVideoIdleTween = null;
            var isBandHighlightVideoDetached = false;

            function getBandHighlightStickyVideo() {
                return bandHighlightStickyVideo;
            }

            function getBandHighlightSourceVideo() {
                return bandHighlightSourceVideo;
            }

            function getBandHighlightTarget() {
                return bandHighlightTarget;
            }

            function getBandHighlightCenterShiftX() {
                var target = getBandHighlightTarget();
                if (!target) return 0;

                var targetRect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
                if (!targetRect.width) return 0;

                var targetCenterX = targetRect.left + (targetRect.width / 2);
                var viewportCenterX = window.innerWidth / 2;

                return viewportCenterX - targetCenterX;
            }

            function getBandContCenterAlignXPercent(selector, direction) {
                var cont = document.querySelector(selector);
                if (!cont) return 0;

                var contRect = cont.getBoundingClientRect();
                var contWidth = contRect.width || cont.offsetWidth || 0;
                if (!contWidth) return 0;

                return (getBandHighlightCenterShiftX() / contWidth) * 100 * direction;
            }

            function applyBandHighlightVideoMotionState() {
                var video = getBandHighlightStickyVideo();
                if (!video) return;

                var props = {
                    x: bandHighlightVideoMotionState.x,
                    y: bandHighlightVideoMotionState.y + bandHighlightVideoIdleState.y,
                    rotation: bandHighlightVideoMotionState.rotation + bandHighlightVideoIdleState.rotation,
                    scale: bandHighlightVideoIdleState.scale,
                    transformOrigin: '50% 50%'
                };
                 
                if (parseFloat(video.style.width) !== bandHighlightVideoMotionState.width) props.width = bandHighlightVideoMotionState.width;
                if (parseFloat(video.style.height) !== bandHighlightVideoMotionState.height) props.height = bandHighlightVideoMotionState.height;
                gsap.set(video, props);
            }

            function stopBandHighlightVideoIdleMotion() {
                if (bandHighlightVideoIdleTween) {
                    bandHighlightVideoIdleTween.kill();
                    bandHighlightVideoIdleTween = null;
                }
                bandHighlightVideoIdleState.y = 0;
                bandHighlightVideoIdleState.rotation = 0;
                bandHighlightVideoIdleState.scale = 1;
                applyBandHighlightVideoMotionState();
            }

            function startBandHighlightVideoIdleMotion() {
                stopBandHighlightVideoIdleMotion();
                bandHighlightVideoIdleTween = gsap.to(bandHighlightVideoIdleState, {
                    y: -18,
                    rotation: 1.2,
                    scale: 1.03,
                    duration: 1.8,
                    ease: 'sine.inOut',
                    repeat: -1,
                    yoyo: true,
                    onUpdate: applyBandHighlightVideoMotionState
                });
            }

            function syncBandHighlightVideoRect() {
                if (isBandHighlightVideoDetached) return;

                var target = bandHighlightSourceVideo;
                var targetBand = bandHighlightTargetBand;
                var layer = bandHighlightLayer;
                var video = bandHighlightStickyVideo;
                if (!target || !layer || !video) return;

                var targetRect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
                var layerRect = layer.getBoundingClientRect();
                if (!targetRect.width || !targetRect.height) return;
                var targetWidth = target.offsetWidth || targetRect.width;
                var targetHeight = target.offsetHeight || targetRect.height;
                var rotation = 0;
                if (targetBand) {
                    var matrix = window.getComputedStyle(targetBand).transform;
                    if (matrix && matrix !== 'none') {
                        var values = matrix.match(/matrix(?:3d)?\(([^)]+)\)/);
                        if (values && values[1]) {
                            var parts = values[1].split(',').map(function (value) {
                                return parseFloat(value);
                            });
                            if (parts.length >= 2 && !isNaN(parts[0]) && !isNaN(parts[1])) {
                                rotation = Math.atan2(parts[1], parts[0]) * (180 / Math.PI);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                bandHighlightVideoMotionState.x = targetRect.left + targetRect.width / 2 - layerRect.left - targetWidth / 2;
                bandHighlightVideoMotionState.y = targetRect.top + targetRect.height / 2 - layerRect.top - targetHeight / 2;
                bandHighlightVideoMotionState.width = targetWidth;
                bandHighlightVideoMotionState.height = targetHeight;
                bandHighlightVideoMotionState.rotation = rotation;
                applyBandHighlightVideoMotionState();
            }

            function getBandHighlightVideoCenterState() {
                var target = document.querySelector('.order_now_sect .band_slide_cont .band.type1 .cont .img.highlight video');
                var layer = document.querySelector('.order_now_sect .band_slide_cont .band_highlight_sticky');
                if (!target || !layer) return {};

                var targetRect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
                var layerRect = layer.getBoundingClientRect();
                var centerScale = window.innerWidth <= 767 ? 1.45 : (window.innerWidth <= 1024 ? 1.7 : 2.5);
                var targetWidth = (target.offsetWidth || targetRect.width) * centerScale;
                var targetHeight = (target.offsetHeight || targetRect.height) * centerScale;
                if (!targetWidth || !targetHeight) return {};

                 
                 
                 
                 
                 
                 
                 
                 
                var layerStyle = window.getComputedStyle(layer);
                var layerTop = layerStyle.position === 'sticky' ? (parseFloat(layerStyle.top) || 0) : layerRect.top;

                return {
                    x: window.innerWidth / 2 - layerRect.left - targetWidth / 2,
                    y: window.innerHeight / 2 - layerTop - targetHeight / 2,
                    width: targetWidth,
                    height: targetHeight,
                    rotation: 0,
                    transformOrigin: '50% 50%'
                };
            }

             
             
             
            window.__bandHighlightCenterState = getBandHighlightVideoCenterState;

            function cacheBandHighlightVideoCenterState() {
                bandHighlightVideoCenterState = getBandHighlightVideoCenterState();
            }

            function captureBandHighlightVideoMotionState() {
                var video = getBandHighlightStickyVideo();
                if (!video) return;

                bandHighlightVideoMotionState.x = parseFloat(gsap.getProperty(video, 'x')) || 0;
                bandHighlightVideoMotionState.y = parseFloat(gsap.getProperty(video, 'y')) || 0;
                bandHighlightVideoMotionState.width = parseFloat(gsap.getProperty(video, 'width')) || video.offsetWidth || 0;
                bandHighlightVideoMotionState.height = parseFloat(gsap.getProperty(video, 'height')) || video.offsetHeight || 0;
                bandHighlightVideoMotionState.rotation = parseFloat(gsap.getProperty(video, 'rotation')) || 0;
            }

            function refreshBandHighlightVideoLayout() {
                if (isBandHighlightVideoDetached) {
                    cacheBandHighlightVideoCenterState();
                    bandHighlightVideoMotionState.x = bandHighlightVideoCenterState.x || 0;
                    bandHighlightVideoMotionState.y = bandHighlightVideoCenterState.y || 0;
                    bandHighlightVideoMotionState.width = bandHighlightVideoCenterState.width || 0;
                    bandHighlightVideoMotionState.height = bandHighlightVideoCenterState.height || 0;
                    bandHighlightVideoMotionState.rotation = typeof bandHighlightVideoCenterState.rotation === 'number' ? bandHighlightVideoCenterState.rotation : 0;
                    applyBandHighlightVideoMotionState();
                    return;
                }

                syncBandHighlightVideoRect();
            }

            function resetBandHighlightVideos() {
                isBandHighlightVideoDetached = false;
                stopBandHighlightVideoIdleMotion();
                getBandHighlightVideos().forEach(function (video) {
                    video.pause();
                    try {
                        video.currentTime = 0;
                    } catch (e) { }
                });

                var sourceVideo = getBandHighlightSourceVideo();
                var stickyVideo = getBandHighlightStickyVideo();

                if (sourceVideo) {
                    gsap.set(sourceVideo, {
                        opacity: 0
                    });
                }

                if (stickyVideo) {
                    gsap.set(stickyVideo, {
                        opacity: 0
                    });
                }
            }

            function playBandHighlightVideos() {
                getBandHighlightVideos().forEach(function (video) {
                    video.muted = true;
                    video.loop = false;
                    video.playsInline = true;
                    video.preload = 'auto';
                    video.play().catch(function () { });
                });
            }

            var isBandHighlightRevealActive = false;

            function setBandHighlightReveal(active) {
                var highlightImg = document.querySelector('.order_now_sect .band_slide_cont .band .cont .img.highlight img');
                var highlightVideo = getBandHighlightSourceVideo();
                var stickyVideo = getBandHighlightStickyVideo();
                if (!highlightImg || !stickyVideo || !highlightVideo || isBandHighlightRevealActive === active) return;

                isBandHighlightRevealActive = active;

                if (active) {
                    stopBandHighlightVideoIdleMotion();
                    isBandHighlightVideoDetached = false;
                    syncBandHighlightVideoRect();
                    playBandHighlightVideos();
                    gsap.to(highlightImg, {
                        opacity: 0,
                        duration: 0.35,
                        ease: 'power1.out',
                        overwrite: true
                    });
                    gsap.to(highlightVideo, {
                        opacity: 1,
                        duration: 0.2,
                        ease: 'power1.out',
                        overwrite: true
                    });
                    gsap.to(stickyVideo, {
                        opacity: 1,
                        duration: 0.35,
                        ease: 'power1.out',
                        overwrite: true
                    });
                    return;
                }

                stopBandHighlightVideoIdleMotion();
                isBandHighlightVideoDetached = false;
                gsap.to(highlightImg, {
                    opacity: 1,
                    duration: 0.25,
                    ease: 'power1.out',
                    overwrite: true
                });
                gsap.to(highlightVideo, {
                    opacity: 0,
                    duration: 0.2,
                    ease: 'power1.out',
                    overwrite: true
                });
                gsap.to(stickyVideo, {
                    opacity: 0,
                    duration: 0.25,
                    ease: 'power1.out',
                    overwrite: true,
                    onComplete: resetBandHighlightVideos
                });
            }

            resetBandHighlightVideos();

            if (enableOrderNowCompactTimeline) {

                 
                 
                var orderNowCards = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('.order_now_sect .slide .swiper-slide'));
                var orderNowKeword = document.querySelector('.order_now_sect .keword_cont');
                var orderNowCardProxies = orderNowCards.map(function () { return { e: 0 }; });
                var orderNowSwiperProxy = { progress: 0 };
                var orderNowCardMeasures = null;
                var orderNowCompactPx = 0;
                var orderNowMoveY = 0;
                var orderNowMaxFullH = 0;

                function getOrderNowSwiper() {
                    return orderNowSwiperEl && orderNowSwiperEl.swiper ? orderNowSwiperEl.swiper : window.orderNowSwiper;
                }

                function applyOrderNowSwiperProgress() {
                    var swiper = getOrderNowSwiper();
                    if (!swiper || swiper.destroyed || typeof swiper.setProgress !== 'function') return;

                    swiper.setProgress(orderNowSwiperProxy.progress, 0);
                }

                function setOrderNowHoverDisabled(disabled) {
                    if (orderNowRoot.classList.contains('is-order-compacting') === disabled) return;

                    orderNowRoot.classList.toggle('is-order-compacting', disabled);
                    orderNowSlide.classList.toggle('is-hover-disabled', disabled);

                    if (disabled) {
                        orderNowCards.forEach(function (card) {
                            var bg = card.querySelector('.bg');
                            card.orderNowHoverLocked = false;
                            if (bg) {
                                gsap.killTweensOf(bg);
                                gsap.set(bg, { x: 0, y: 0 });
                            }
                        });
                    }
                }

                function measureOrderNowCards() {
                     
                     
                     
                    orderNowTrigger.style.height = '';
                    orderNowSticky.style.height = '';
                    var orderNowBoxH = orderNowTrigger.offsetHeight;
                    var orderNowStickyH = orderNowSticky.offsetHeight;
                    orderNowTrigger.style.height = orderNowBoxH + 'px';
                    orderNowSticky.style.height = orderNowStickyH + 'px';
                    var rem = parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).fontSize) || 10;
                    orderNowCompactPx = 15 * rem;
                    orderNowMoveY = getOrderNowSlideMoveY();
                    orderNowMaxFullH = 0;
                    orderNowCardMeasures = orderNowCards.map(function (el) {
                        var tit = el.querySelector('.txt_box .tit');
                        var desc = el.querySelector('.txt_box .desc');
                        var icoImg = el.querySelector('.ico img');
                        var bg = el.querySelector('.bg');
                        var bgStyles = bg ? window.getComputedStyle(bg) : null;
                        var bgPadTop = bgStyles ? (parseFloat(bgStyles.paddingTop) || 0) : 0;
                        var bgPadBottom = bgStyles ? (parseFloat(bgStyles.paddingBottom) || 0) : 0;
                        var titHeight = tit ? tit.offsetHeight : 0;
                        var titOffsetTop = tit ? tit.offsetTop : 0;
                        if (icoImg) icoImg.style.transformOrigin = 'right bottom';
                        var m = {
                            bg: bg,
                            tit: tit,
                            txt: el.querySelector('.txt_box .txt'),
                            icoImg: icoImg,
                            fullH: el.offsetHeight,
                            bgPadTop: bgPadTop,
                            bgPadBottom: bgPadBottom,
                            titHeight: titHeight,
                            titOffsetTop: titOffsetTop,
                            descH: desc ? desc.offsetHeight : 0,
                            titMT: tit ? (parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(tit).marginTop) || 0) : 0
                        };
                        if (m.fullH > orderNowMaxFullH) orderNowMaxFullH = m.fullH;
                        return m;
                    });
                }

                function applyOrderNowCard(i) {
                    if (!orderNowCardMeasures) measureOrderNowCards();
                    var m = orderNowCardMeasures[i];
                    if (!m) return;
                    var e = orderNowCardProxies[i].e;
                    var compactVisibleH = m.bgPadTop + m.bgPadBottom + m.titHeight;
                    var dh = Math.max(0, ((m.fullH - compactVisibleH) - m.bgPadBottom)) * e;
                    var lift = -(Math.max(0, m.titOffsetTop - m.bgPadTop)) * e;

                    m.bg.style.clipPath = dh > 0.5 ? 'inset(0 0 ' + dh + 'px 0 round 3rem)' : '';
                    if (m.tit) m.tit.style.transform = 'translate3d(0,' + lift + 'px,0)';
                    if (m.txt) m.txt.style.transform = 'translate3d(0,' + lift + 'px,0)';
                    if (m.icoImg) m.icoImg.style.transform = 'translate3d(0,' + (-dh) + 'px,0) scale(' + (1 - e) + ')';
                }

                function applyOrderNowAll() {
                    for (var i = 0; i < orderNowCardProxies.length; i++) applyOrderNowCard(i);
                }

                ScrollTrigger.addEventListener('refresh', function () {
                    measureOrderNowCards();
                    applyOrderNowSwiperProgress();
                    applyOrderNowAll();
                });


                var orderNowTimeline = gsap.timeline({
                    onComplete: function () {
                        setOrderNowCompactComplete(true);
                    },
                    onReverseComplete: function () {
                        setOrderNowCompactComplete(false);
                    },
                    scrollTrigger: {
                        trigger: orderNowTrigger,
                        start: 'top top',
                        end: 'bottom bottom',
                        scrub: true,
                        markers: false,
                        invalidateOnRefresh: true,
                    }
                });

                orderNowTimeline
                    .to('.delay', { duration: 0.6 })
                    .to(orderNowSwiperProxy, {
                        progress: 1,
                        ease: 'none',
                        duration: 1,
                        onUpdate: applyOrderNowSwiperProgress
                    })
                    .addLabel('orderNowCardCompact')
                    .to('.order_now_sect .sect_tit_box', {
                        y: "-50vh",
                        ease: 'none',
                        duration: 1,
                    }, 'orderNowCardCompact')
                    .to('.order_now_sect .slide', {
                        y: getOrderNowSlideMoveY,
                        ease: 'none',
                        duration: 1.5,
                    }, 'orderNowCardCompact')
                    .to('.order_now_sect .keword_cont', {
                        y: getOrderNowKewordMoveY,
                        ease: 'none',
                        duration: 1.5,
                    }, 'orderNowCardCompact')
                    .to('.order_now_sect .slide .swiper-slide .txt_box .desc', {
                        opacity: 0,
                        ease: 'power1.out',
                        duration: 0.4,
                        stagger: getOrderNowCardDelay,
                    }, 'orderNowCardCompact')

                    .to('.order_now_sect .slide .swiper-slide .txt_box .txt', {
                        opacity: 0,
                        paddingTop: "0",
                        marginTop: "-2rem",
                        ease: 'none',
                        duration: 0.4,
                        stagger: getOrderNowCardDelay,
                    }, 'orderNowCardCompact')
                    .to('.order_now_sect .slide .swiper-slide .ico', {
                        opacity: 0,
                        ease: 'power1.out',
                        duration: 0.4,
                        stagger: getOrderNowCardDelay,
                    }, 'orderNowCardCompact');

                orderNowCards.forEach(function (card, i) {
                    orderNowTimeline.to(orderNowCardProxies[i], {
                        e: 1,
                        ease: 'none',
                        duration: 1,
                        onUpdate: (function (idx) {
                            return function () {
                                applyOrderNowCard(idx);
                            };
                        })(i)
                    }, 'orderNowCardCompact+=' + getOrderNowCardDelay(i));
                });

                orderNowTimeline
                    .addLabel('orderNowKewordHold')
                    .to('.delay', {
                        duration: 1.6,
                    });

                orderNowTimeline.eventCallback('onUpdate', function () {
                    setOrderNowHoverDisabled(orderNowTimeline.time() >= orderNowTimeline.labels.orderNowCardCompact);
                });

            }

            window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
                requestAnimationFrame(refreshBandHighlightVideoLayout);
            });




            if (bandSlideCont) {
                var bandHighlightMotionHold = { progress: 0 };
                gsap.set('.order_now_sect .band_slide_cont', {
                    xPercent: 0
                });
                refreshBandHighlightVideoLayout();

                var bandTimeline = gsap.timeline({
                    scrollTrigger: {
                        trigger: bandSlideCont,
                        start: 'top bottom',
                        end: 'bottom bottom',
                        scrub: true,
                        invalidateOnRefresh: true,
                        onLeaveBack: function () {
                            setBandHighlightReveal(false);
                            resetBandHighlightVideos();
                        },
                        onRefresh: refreshBandHighlightVideoLayout,
                        markers: false
                    }
                })
                    .to('.order_now_sect .band_slide_cont .band.type1 .cont', {
                        xPercent: function () {
                            return getBandContCenterAlignXPercent('.order_now_sect .band_slide_cont .band.type1 .cont', 1);
                        },
                        ease: 'none',
                        onUpdate: syncBandHighlightVideoRect
                    }, 0)
                    .to('.order_now_sect .band_slide_cont .band.type2 .cont', {
                        xPercent: function () {
                            return getBandContCenterAlignXPercent('.order_now_sect .band_slide_cont .band.type2 .cont', -1);
                        },
                        ease: 'none'
                    }, 0)
                    .addLabel('bandHighlightReveal')
                    .to(bandHighlightMotionHold, {
                        progress: 1,
                        duration: 0.18,
                        ease: 'none'
                    })
                    .call(function () {
                        captureBandHighlightVideoMotionState();
                        cacheBandHighlightVideoCenterState();
                        isBandHighlightVideoDetached = true;
                    })
                    .to(bandHighlightVideoMotionState, {
                        x: function () { return bandHighlightVideoCenterState.x; },
                        y: function () { return bandHighlightVideoCenterState.y; },
                        width: function () { return bandHighlightVideoCenterState.width; },
                        height: function () { return bandHighlightVideoCenterState.height; },
                        rotation: function () { return bandHighlightVideoCenterState.rotation; },
                        ease: 'none',
                        duration: 0.25,
                        onUpdate: applyBandHighlightVideoMotionState,
                        onComplete: startBandHighlightVideoIdleMotion,
                        onReverseComplete: stopBandHighlightVideoIdleMotion
                    })
                    .to('.order_now_sect .band_slide_cont .band.type1 .cont', {
                        yPercent: -100,
                        duration: 0.1,
                    }, ">-0.2")
                    .to('.order_now_sect .band_slide_cont .band.type1 .bg ', {
                        yPercent: -100,
                        duration: 0.1,
                    }, "<")
                    .to('.order_now_sect .band_slide_cont .band.type2 .cont', {
                        yPercent: 100,
                        duration: 0.1,
                    }, "<")
                    .to('.order_now_sect .band_slide_cont .band.type2 .bg ', {
                        yPercent: 100,
                        duration: 0.12,
                    }, "<")
                    .to('.order_now_sect .band_slide_cont .moveText', {
                        opacity: 0,
                        ease: 'none',
                        duration: 0.1,
                    }, ">-0.1")

                    .to('.order_now_sect .since2_box', {
                        opacity: 1,
                        duration: 0.3,
                        ease: "power1.in"
                    }, ">-0.3")
                    .to(orderNowSince2TitleChars, {
                        yPercent: 0,
                        filter: 'blur(0px)',
                        opacity: 1,
                        duration: 0.05,
                        stagger: 0.02,
                    }, ">-0.05")
                    .to('.order_now_sect .since2_box .txt', {
                        opacity: 1,
                        y: 0,
                        duration: 0.1,
                        ease: "none"
                    }, ">-0.05")




                    .to('.delay', {
                        duration: 0.3,
                    })
                    .to('.order_now_sect .band_highlight_sticky ', { top: "-100svh", duration: 0.6 })
                    .to(orderNowSince2TitleChars, {
                        yPercent: -30,
                        filter: 'blur(10px)',
                        opacity: 0,
                        duration: 0.04,
                        // stagger: function (index, target) {
                        //     var randomSeed = parseFloat(target.style.getPropertyValue('--char-random')) || 0;
                        //     return (index * 0.012) + (randomSeed * 0.003);
                        // },
                        stagger: 0.01,
                        ease: "power2.in"
                    }, "<")
                    .to('.order_now_sect .since2_box .main_in .txt', {
                        y: "-100%",
                        opacity: "0",
                        duration: 0.45,
                    }, "<")

                    .to('.order_now_sect .since2_box .main_in .tit', {
                        y: "-100%",
                        duration: 0.45,
                        ease: "none"
                    }, "<")

                    .to('.order_now_sect .gra_bg_wrap ', { top: "0", duration: 1, opacity: 1 }, ">-0.3")

                    .to('.order_now_sect .gra_bg_wrap .gradient.w100', {
                        y: "-110vh",
                        duration: 0.35,
                        ease: "none",
                        onUpdate: function () {
                            $('.order_now_sect .since2_box').toggleClass('wh', this.progress() >= 0.75);
                        }
                    }, "<")
                    .to('.order_now_sect .gra_bg_wrap .gradient.w78', { y: "-110vh", duration: 0.3, ease: "none" }, "<+0.045")
                    .to('.order_now_sect .gra_bg_wrap .gradient.w52', { y: "-110vh", duration: 0.25, ease: "none" }, "<+0.045")
                    .to('.order_now_sect .gra_bg_wrap .gradient.w26', { y: "-110vh", duration: 0.2, ease: "none" }, "<+0.045")
                    .to('.order_now_sect .gra_bg_wrap .gra_bg_cont .bg_color', {
                        opacity: 1,
                        duration: 0.1,
                    }, ">-0.01")




                // .to('.active', {
                //     onStart: () => { $('.order_now_sect .since2_box').addClass('active') },

                bandTimeline.eventCallback('onUpdate', function () {
                    setBandHighlightReveal(bandTimeline.time() >= bandTimeline.labels.bandHighlightReveal);
                });
                //     onReverseComplete: () => { $('.order_now_sect .since2_box').removeClass('active') },
                // }, ">-0.15")
            }


        }

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
                initOrderNowSwiper();
                initOrderNowHoverMotion();
                initOrderNowScrollTrigger();
            });
        } else {
            initOrderNowSwiper();
            initOrderNowHoverMotion();
            initOrderNowScrollTrigger();
        }
    })();

    (function () {
        function getCssLengthPx(el, name, fallback) {
            var value = window.getComputedStyle(el).getPropertyValue(name).trim();
            var num = parseFloat(value);

            if (!value || isNaN(num)) return fallback;
            if (value.indexOf('px') > -1) return num;

            var tester = document.createElement('div');
            tester.style.position = 'absolute';
            tester.style.visibility = 'hidden';
            tester.style.width = value;
            el.appendChild(tester);

            var px = parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(tester).width);
            el.removeChild(tester);

            if (!isNaN(px)) return px;

            return num;
        }

        function initProcessSwiper() {
            if (typeof Swiper === 'undefined') return;

            var processSect = document.querySelector('.process_sect');
            var processSwiperEl = document.querySelector('.process_sect .cont_box .slide .swiper-container');
            var processSlideEl = processSwiperEl ? processSwiperEl.closest('.slide') : null;
            var processContBox = processSlideEl ? processSlideEl.closest('.cont_box') : null;
            var motorcycleEl = processContBox ? processContBox.querySelector('.motorcycle') : null;
            var processBanner = processSect ? processSect.querySelector('.process_banner') : null;
            var header = document.querySelector('.header');
            var motorcycleTargetX = 0;
            var motorcycleCurrentX = 0;
            var motorcycleFrame = null;
            var motorcycleReady = false;
            var processSwiper = null;
            var lastProcessProgress = 0;
            var processScrollTrigger = null;
            var PROCESS_SWIPER_START_PROGRESS = 0.3;
            var PROCESS_BANNER_START_PROGRESS = 0.85;

            if (!processSect || !processSwiperEl || processSwiperEl.swiper || !processSlideEl) return;
            if (window.innerWidth <= 1040) {
                var mobileLastIndex = 0;

                function setMobileMotorcycleProgress(swiper, immediate) {
                    if (!motorcycleEl || !swiper || !swiper.slides || !swiper.slides.length) return;

                    var maxIndex = Math.max(swiper.slides.length - 1, 1);
                    var activeIndex = typeof swiper.activeIndex === 'number' ? swiper.activeIndex : 0;
                    var progress = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, activeIndex / maxIndex));
                    var motoWidth = motorcycleEl.offsetWidth || 0;
                    var targetX = ((window.innerWidth * 0.9) - motoWidth) * progress;

                    if (!immediate) {
                        if (activeIndex < mobileLastIndex) {
                            motorcycleEl.classList.add('is-reverse');
                        } else if (activeIndex > mobileLastIndex) {
                            motorcycleEl.classList.remove('is-reverse');
                        }
                    }

                    motorcycleEl.style.transform = 'translate3d(' + targetX.toFixed(3) + 'px, 0, 0)';
                    mobileLastIndex = activeIndex;
                }

                new Swiper(processSwiperEl, {
                    slidesPerView: 'auto',
                    spaceBetween: 0,
                    freeMode: false,
                    allowTouchMove: true,
                    simulateTouch: true,
                    grabCursor: true,
                    mousewheel: false,
                    keyboard: false,
                    on: {
                        init: function () {
                            setMobileMotorcycleProgress(this, true);
                        },
                        slideChangeTransitionStart: function () {
                            setMobileMotorcycleProgress(this, false);
                        },
                        resize: function () {
                            setMobileMotorcycleProgress(this, true);
                        }
                    }
                });
                return;
            }
            if (typeof ScrollTrigger === 'undefined' || typeof gsap === 'undefined') return;

            gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);

            function clampProgress(value) {
                return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, value || 0));
            }

            function getProcessSwiperProgress(rawProgress) {
                return clampProgress((rawProgress - PROCESS_SWIPER_START_PROGRESS) / (PROCESS_BANNER_START_PROGRESS - PROCESS_SWIPER_START_PROGRESS));
            }

            function setProcessBannerActive(rawProgress) {
                if (!processBanner) return;

                processBanner.classList.toggle('active', rawProgress >= PROCESS_BANNER_START_PROGRESS);
            }

            function getHeaderHeightOffset() {
                var hdHValue = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--hdH').trim();
                if (!hdHValue) return header ? header.offsetHeight : 0;

                var measureEl = document.createElement('div');
                measureEl.style.position = 'absolute';
                measureEl.style.visibility = 'hidden';
                measureEl.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
                measureEl.style.height = hdHValue;
                document.body.appendChild(measureEl);

                var height = measureEl.getBoundingClientRect().height;
                document.body.removeChild(measureEl);

                return height;
            }

            function setProcessSlideOn(rawProgress) {
                var slides = Array.prototype.slice.call(processSwiperEl.querySelectorAll('.swiper-slide'));
                var lastIndex = slides.length - 1;
                var activeProgress = clampProgress(rawProgress / 0.8);
                var activeIndex = lastIndex > 0 ? Math.floor(activeProgress * lastIndex + 0.0001) : 0;

                slides.forEach(function (slide, index) {
                    slide.classList.toggle('on', index <= activeIndex);
                });
            }

            function getMotorcycleTargetX() {
                if (!motorcycleEl) return 0;

                 
                var motoWidth = motorcycleWidth !== null ? motorcycleWidth : motorcycleEl.offsetWidth;
                return (window.innerWidth * 0.9) - motoWidth;
            }

            function setMotorcycleX(x) {
                if (!motorcycleEl) return;

                motorcycleEl.style.transform = 'translate3d(' + x.toFixed(3) + 'px, 0, 0)';
            }

            function setMotorcycleDirection(progress, immediate) {
                if (!motorcycleEl) return;

                if (!immediate) {
                    if (progress < lastProcessProgress - 0.0005) {
                        motorcycleEl.classList.add('is-reverse');
                    } else if (progress > lastProcessProgress + 0.0005) {
                        motorcycleEl.classList.remove('is-reverse');
                    }
                }

                lastProcessProgress = progress;
            }

            function animateMotorcycle() {
                var diff = motorcycleTargetX - motorcycleCurrentX;

                if (Math.abs(diff) < 0.25) {
                    motorcycleCurrentX = motorcycleTargetX;
                    setMotorcycleX(motorcycleCurrentX);
                    motorcycleFrame = null;
                    return;
                }

                motorcycleCurrentX += diff * 0.07;
                setMotorcycleX(motorcycleCurrentX);
                motorcycleFrame = requestAnimationFrame(animateMotorcycle);
            }

            function updateMotorcycle(swiper, immediate) {
                if (!motorcycleEl) return;

                var progress = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, swiper.progress || 0));
                motorcycleTargetX = getMotorcycleTargetX() * progress;
                setMotorcycleDirection(progress, immediate);

                if (immediate || !motorcycleReady) {
                    motorcycleReady = true;
                    motorcycleCurrentX = motorcycleTargetX;
                    setMotorcycleX(motorcycleCurrentX);
                    return;
                }

                if (!motorcycleFrame) {
                    motorcycleFrame = requestAnimationFrame(animateMotorcycle);
                }
            }

             
            var motorcycleWidth = null;

            function measureMotorcycleWidth() {
                motorcycleWidth = motorcycleEl ? motorcycleEl.getBoundingClientRect().width : null;
            }

            function syncProcessByProgress(rawProgress, immediate) {
                var progress = clampProgress(rawProgress);

                processSwiper.setProgress(getProcessSwiperProgress(progress), 0);
                setProcessSlideOn(getProcessSwiperProgress(progress));
                updateMotorcycle(processSwiper, immediate);
                setProcessBannerActive(progress);
            }

            processSwiper = new Swiper(processSwiperEl, {
                slidesPerView: 'auto',
                spaceBetween: 0,
                freeMode: false,
                allowTouchMove: false,
                simulateTouch: false,
                grabCursor: false,
                mousewheel: false,
                keyboard: false,
                speed: 0,
                on: {
                    init: function () {
                        setProcessSlideOn(this.progress || 0);
                        updateMotorcycle(this, true);
                    },
                    progress: function () {
                        setProcessSlideOn(this.progress || 0);
                        updateMotorcycle(this);
                    },
                    setTranslate: function () {
                        setProcessSlideOn(this.progress || 0);
                        updateMotorcycle(this);
                    }
                }
            });

            setProcessBannerActive(0);

            processScrollTrigger = ScrollTrigger.create({
                trigger: processSect,
                start: 'top top',
                end: 'bottom bottom',
                scrub: true,
                invalidateOnRefresh: true,
                onUpdate: function (self) {
                    syncProcessByProgress(self.progress || 0);
                },
                onRefresh: function (self) {
                    processSwiper.update();
                    measureMotorcycleWidth();
                    syncProcessByProgress(self.progress || 0, true);
                }
            });

            ScrollTrigger.create({
                trigger: processSect,
                start: function () {
                    return 'top+=' + getHeaderHeightOffset() + ' top';
                },
                end: 'bottom top',
                invalidateOnRefresh: true,
                onEnter: function () {
                    if (header) header.classList.add('color_type');
                },
                onEnterBack: function () {
                    if (header) header.classList.add('color_type');
                },
                onLeaveBack: function () {
                    if (header) header.classList.remove('color_type');
                }
            });

            window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
                processSwiper.update();
                measureMotorcycleWidth();
                if (processScrollTrigger) {
                    syncProcessByProgress(processScrollTrigger.progress || 0);
                }
            });

            if (window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh) {
                window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh([0, 120, 360]);
            }
        }

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initProcessSwiper);
        } else {
            initProcessSwiper();
        }
    })();

    (function () {
        function initSpecialCardMouseMove() {
            var cardWrap = document.querySelector('.special_sect .card_wrap');
            var cardList = cardWrap ? cardWrap.querySelector('.first_card_list') : null;
            var cardItems = cardWrap ? cardWrap.querySelectorAll('.first_card_list .item') : [];
            var pointerHistory = [];
            var hoverPushEnabled = false;
            if (!cardWrap || !cardList) return;

            function clamp(value, min, max) {
                return Math.min(Math.max(value, min), max);
            }

            function trackPointer(event) {
                if (!hoverPushEnabled) return;
                if (event.pointerType && event.pointerType !== 'mouse') return;

                pointerHistory.push({
                    x: event.clientX,
                    y: event.clientY,
                    time: Date.now()
                });

                if (pointerHistory.length > 4) {
                    pointerHistory.shift();
                }
            }

            function resetCardPush(item) {
                if (item.hoverPushRaf) {
                    window.cancelAnimationFrame(item.hoverPushRaf);
                    item.hoverPushRaf = null;
                }

                item.style.setProperty('--card-hover-push-x', '0px');
                item.style.setProperty('--card-hover-push-y', '0px');
                item.style.setProperty('--card-hover-push-rotate', '0deg');
                item.classList.remove('is-hover-pushed');
            }

            function getEdgeVector(item, event) {
                var rect = item.getBoundingClientRect();
                var distances = [
                    { x: 0, y: 1, value: Math.abs(event.clientY - rect.top) },
                    { x: 0, y: -1, value: Math.abs(rect.bottom - event.clientY) },
                    { x: 1, y: 0, value: Math.abs(event.clientX - rect.left) },
                    { x: -1, y: 0, value: Math.abs(rect.right - event.clientX) }
                ];

                distances.sort(function (a, b) {
                    return a.value - b.value;
                });

                return distances[0];
            }

            function pushCard(item, event) {
                if (!hoverPushEnabled) return;
                if (event.pointerType && event.pointerType !== 'mouse') return;
                 
                 
                 
                if (cardWrap.closest('.special_sect').classList.contains('is-riding')) return;
                if (cardWrap.classList.contains('is-gathering') && !cardWrap.classList.contains('is-gather-complete')) return;
                if (item.classList.contains('fixed') && !cardList.classList.contains('active')) return;
                if (item.classList.contains('is-hover-pushed')) return;

                var current = { x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY, time: Date.now() };
                var previous = null;

                for (var index = pointerHistory.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
                    var candidate = pointerHistory[index];
                    var candidateDistance = Math.hypot(current.x - candidate.x, current.y - candidate.y);

                    if (candidateDistance > 2) {
                        previous = candidate;
                        break;
                    }
                }

                var edgeVector = getEdgeVector(item, event);
                var dx = previous ? current.x - previous.x : edgeVector.x;
                var dy = previous ? current.y - previous.y : edgeVector.y;
                var distance = Math.sqrt((dx * dx) + (dy * dy));
                if (distance < 1) {
                    dx = edgeVector.x;
                    dy = edgeVector.y;
                    distance = 1;
                }

                var maxPower = 200;
                var maxMove = 200;
                var spring = 0.01;
                var damping = 0.9;
                var position = { x: 0, y: 0 };
                var velocity = {
                    x: clamp((dx / window.innerWidth) * maxPower, -maxMove, maxMove),
                    y: clamp((dy / window.innerHeight) * maxPower, -maxMove, maxMove)
                };

                resetCardPush(item);
                item.classList.add('is-hover-pushed');

                function animatePush() {
                    velocity.x += -position.x * spring;
                    velocity.y += -position.y * spring;
                    velocity.x *= damping;
                    velocity.y *= damping;
                    position.x += velocity.x;
                    position.y += velocity.y;
                    position.x = clamp(position.x, -maxMove, maxMove);
                    position.y = clamp(position.y, -maxMove, maxMove);

                    item.style.setProperty('--card-hover-push-x', position.x.toFixed(2) + 'px');
                    item.style.setProperty('--card-hover-push-y', position.y.toFixed(2) + 'px');
                    item.style.setProperty('--card-hover-push-rotate', clamp(position.x * 0.06, -8, 8).toFixed(2) + 'deg');

                    if (Math.hypot(velocity.x, velocity.y) > 0.05 || Math.hypot(position.x, position.y) > 0.5) {
                        item.hoverPushRaf = window.requestAnimationFrame(animatePush);
                        return;
                    }

                    item.hoverPushRaf = null;
                    item.style.setProperty('--card-hover-push-x', '0px');
                    item.style.setProperty('--card-hover-push-y', '0px');
                    item.style.setProperty('--card-hover-push-rotate', '0deg');
                    item.classList.remove('is-hover-pushed');
                }

                item.hoverPushRaf = window.requestAnimationFrame(animatePush);
            }

            document.addEventListener('pointermove', trackPointer, { passive: true });

            cardItems.forEach(function (item) {
                item.addEventListener('pointerenter', function (event) {
                    pushCard(item, event);
                });
            });

            cardWrap.addEventListener('mouseleave', function () {
                cardItems.forEach(function (item) {
                    if (!item.hoverPushRaf) {
                        resetCardPush(item);
                    }
                });
            });
        }

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initSpecialCardMouseMove);
        } else {
            initSpecialCardMouseMove();
        }
    })();

    (function () {
        function initSpecialGatherScrollTrigger() {
            if (typeof gsap === 'undefined' || typeof ScrollTrigger === 'undefined') return;

            var specialSect = document.querySelector('.special_sect');
            var cardWrap = specialSect ? specialSect.querySelector('.card_wrap') : null;
            var firstCardList = cardWrap ? cardWrap.querySelector('.first_card_list') : null;
            var cardItems = cardWrap ? cardWrap.querySelectorAll('.first_card_list .item') : [];
            var specialnessCont = specialSect ? specialSect.querySelector('.specialness_cont') : null;
            var specialIntroTitleBox = document.querySelector('.special_sect > .sticky_box .sect_tit_box');
            if (!specialSect || !cardWrap || !firstCardList || !specialnessCont) return;

            gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);

            function setGatherProgress(progress) {
                var spread = 70 * (1 - progress);
                var rotate = 10 * (1 - progress);

                firstCardList.style.setProperty('--card-left-x', (-spread).toFixed(3) + '%');
                firstCardList.style.setProperty('--card-right-x', spread.toFixed(3) + '%');
                firstCardList.style.setProperty('--card-left-rotate', (-rotate).toFixed(3) + 'deg');
                firstCardList.style.setProperty('--card-right-rotate', rotate.toFixed(3) + 'deg');
            }

            function resetCardMoves() {
                cardItems.forEach(function (item) {
                    item.style.setProperty('--card-move-x', '0px');
                    item.style.setProperty('--card-move-y', '0px');
                });
            }

            function setSpecialnessActive(isActive) {
                var header = document.querySelector('.header');

                specialSect.classList.toggle('specialness-active', isActive);

                if (header) {
                    header.classList.toggle('color_type', !isActive);
                }
            }

            function activateSpecialness() {
                cardWrap.classList.add('is-revealed');
                cardWrap.classList.add('is-gather-complete');
                setGatherProgress(1);
                firstCardList.classList.add('active');
                setSpecialnessActive(true);
                 
                if (typeof window.__spCardCanvas === 'function') window.__spCardCanvas(true);
            }

            var specialIntroResetTimer = null;
            var specialIntroWasReset = false;

            function cancelSpecialIntroReset() {
                if (specialIntroResetTimer) {
                    clearTimeout(specialIntroResetTimer);
                    specialIntroResetTimer = null;
                }

                cardWrap.classList.remove('is-resetting');
            }

            function resetSpecialIntroState() {
                if (specialIntroResetTimer) {
                    clearTimeout(specialIntroResetTimer);
                    specialIntroResetTimer = null;
                }

                if (cardWrap.specialMouseReadyTimer) {
                    clearTimeout(cardWrap.specialMouseReadyTimer);
                    cardWrap.specialMouseReadyTimer = null;
                }

                cardWrap.classList.add('is-resetting');
                if (specialIntroTitleBox) {
                    specialIntroTitleBox.classList.remove('active');
                }
                cardWrap.classList.remove('active');
                cardWrap.classList.remove('is-revealed');
                cardWrap.classList.remove('is-gathering');
                cardWrap.classList.remove('is-gather-complete');
                cardWrap.classList.remove('is-mouse-ready');
                firstCardList.classList.remove('active');
                setSpecialnessActive(false);
                if (typeof window.__spCardCanvas === 'function') window.__spCardCanvas(false);
                resetCardMoves();
                setGatherProgress(0);

                if (typeof window.resetSectBigTitleReveal === 'function') {
                    window.resetSectBigTitleReveal(specialSect);
                }

                specialIntroWasReset = true;

                specialIntroResetTimer = setTimeout(function () {
                    cardWrap.classList.remove('is-resetting');
                    specialIntroResetTimer = null;
                }, 100);
            }

            function restoreSpecialIntroState(options) {
                var settings = options || {};

                cancelSpecialIntroReset();

                if (specialIntroTitleBox) {
                    specialIntroTitleBox.classList.add('active');
                }
                cardWrap.classList.add('active');

                if (typeof cardWrap.specialRevealComplete === 'function') {
                    cardWrap.specialRevealComplete();
                } else {
                    cardWrap.classList.add('is-revealed');
                }

                if (settings.revealBigTitle && specialIntroTitleBox) {
                    gsap.utils.toArray(specialIntroTitleBox.querySelectorAll('.big_tit.is-char-reveal')).forEach(function (title) {
                        if (title.sectBigTitleRevealTween) {
                            title.sectBigTitleRevealTween.restart();
                        }
                    });
                }
            }

            cardWrap.specialRevealComplete = function () {
                cardWrap.classList.add('is-revealed');
            };

            setGatherProgress(0);



            ScrollTrigger.create({
                trigger: specialSect,
                start: 'top bottom',
                end: 'bottom bottom',
                markers: false,
                invalidateOnRefresh: true,
                onEnter: function () {
                    restoreSpecialIntroState({ revealBigTitle: true });
                    specialIntroWasReset = false;
                },
                onEnterBack: function () {
                    restoreSpecialIntroState({ revealBigTitle: true });
                    specialIntroWasReset = false;
                },
                onLeaveBack: function () {
                    resetSpecialIntroState();
                }
            });


            gsap.timeline({
                scrollTrigger: {
                    trigger: '.special_sect .specialness_cont .cont_wrap ',
                    start: 'top bottom',
                    end: 'top top',
                    scrub: true,
                    // markers: true,
                    invalidateOnRefresh: true,
                    onEnter: restoreSpecialIntroState,
                    onEnterBack: restoreSpecialIntroState,

                }
            }).to('.delay', {
                duration: '0.6',
                ease: 'none',
            }).to('.special_sect .sticky_box .sect_tit_box', {
                y: '-100vh',
                ease: 'none',
            }).to('.special_sect .card_wrap', {
                y: '0vh',
                paddingTop: 0,
                ease: 'none',
            }, "<")
                .to(firstCardList, {
                    '--card-left-x': '0%',
                    '--card-right-x': '0%',
                    '--card-left-rotate': '0deg',
                    '--card-right-rotate': '0deg',
                    ease: 'none'
                }, "<")


            gsap.timeline({
                scrollTrigger: {
                    trigger: specialnessCont,
                    start: 'top bottom+=50%',
                    end: 'top top',
                    scrub: true,
                    markers: false,
                    invalidateOnRefresh: true,
                    onEnter: function () {
                        cancelSpecialIntroReset();
                        resetCardMoves();
                        cardWrap.classList.remove('is-gather-complete');
                        cardWrap.classList.add('is-gathering');
                        cardWrap.classList.remove('is-mouse-ready');
                         
                        if (typeof window.__spCardCanvas === 'function') window.__spCardCanvas(false);
                    },
                    onEnterBack: function () {
                        cancelSpecialIntroReset();
                        firstCardList.classList.remove('active');
                        setSpecialnessActive(false);
                        resetCardMoves();
                        cardWrap.classList.remove('is-gather-complete');
                        cardWrap.classList.add('is-gathering');
                        cardWrap.classList.remove('is-mouse-ready');
                        if (typeof window.__spCardCanvas === 'function') window.__spCardCanvas(false);
                    },
                    onLeave: function () {
                        resetCardMoves();
                        activateSpecialness();
                    },
                    onLeaveBack: function () {
                        resetCardMoves();
                        setGatherProgress(0);
                        cardWrap.classList.remove('is-gathering');
                        cardWrap.classList.remove('is-gather-complete');
                        setSpecialnessActive(false);
                        if (typeof window.__spCardCanvas === 'function') window.__spCardCanvas(false);
                    }
                }
            })
        }

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initSpecialGatherScrollTrigger);
        } else {
            initSpecialGatherScrollTrigger();
        }
    })();

    (function () {
        function initSpecialMoStickyScrollTrigger() {
            if (typeof gsap === 'undefined' || typeof ScrollTrigger === 'undefined') return;
            var specialSect = document.querySelector('.special_sect');
            var moSticky = document.querySelector('.special_sect .specialness_cont .cont_wrap .mo_sticky');
            var titleBox = moSticky ? moSticky.querySelector('.sect_tit_box') : null;
            var titleTxt = titleBox ? titleBox.querySelector('.txt') : null;
            var titleTit = titleBox ? titleBox.querySelector('.tit') : null;
            var whBg = specialSect ? specialSect.querySelector('.wh_bg') : null;
            var cardWrap = specialSect ? specialSect.querySelector('.card_wrap') : null;
            if (!specialSect || !moSticky || !titleBox || !titleTxt || !titleTit || !whBg || !cardWrap) return;

            gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);

            function clearSpecialMoStickyInlineStyles() {
                gsap.set([whBg, titleBox, cardWrap], {
                    clearProps: 'clipPath,y,transition'
                });
            }

            function destroySpecialMoStickyTimeline() {
                if (specialSect.__specialMoStickyResizeRaf) {
                    cancelAnimationFrame(specialSect.__specialMoStickyResizeRaf);
                    specialSect.__specialMoStickyResizeRaf = null;
                }
                if (specialSect.__specialMoStickyResizeHandler) {
                    if (window.offLayoutResize) window.offLayoutResize(specialSect.__specialMoStickyResizeHandler);
                    specialSect.__specialMoStickyResizeHandler = null;
                }
                if (specialSect.__specialMoStickyTimeline) {
                    if (specialSect.__specialMoStickyTimeline.scrollTrigger) {
                        specialSect.__specialMoStickyTimeline.scrollTrigger.kill();
                    }
                    specialSect.__specialMoStickyTimeline.kill();
                    specialSect.__specialMoStickyTimeline = null;
                }
            }

            function refreshSpecialMoStickyLayout() {
                var timeline = specialSect.__specialMoStickyTimeline;
                if (!timeline || !timeline.scrollTrigger) return;
                if (window.innerWidth <= 0) {
                    clearSpecialMoStickyInlineStyles();
                    return;
                }

                var scrollTrigger = timeline.scrollTrigger;
                var progress = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, scrollTrigger.progress || 0));
                var clipPathValue = 'inset(0 0 ' + (progress * 100) + '% 0)';
                var titleY = window.innerWidth <= 768 ? 0 : -window.innerHeight * progress;

                moSticky.getBoundingClientRect();
                whBg.getBoundingClientRect();
                cardWrap.getBoundingClientRect();
                titleBox.getBoundingClientRect();

                gsap.set(whBg, {
                    clipPath: clipPathValue
                });
                gsap.set(titleBox, {
                    y: titleY
                });
                gsap.set(cardWrap, {
                    clipPath: clipPathValue
                });
            }

            destroySpecialMoStickyTimeline();

            if (window.innerWidth <= 0) {
                clearSpecialMoStickyInlineStyles();
                return;
            }

            specialSect.__specialMoStickyTimeline = gsap.timeline({
                scrollTrigger: {
                    trigger: moSticky,
                    start: 'top top',
                    end: window.innerWidth <= 768 ? 'bottom top' : 'bottom+=100% top',
                    scrub: true,
                    invalidateOnRefresh: true,
                    onRefresh: refreshSpecialMoStickyLayout,
                    onLeave: function () {
                        specialSect.classList.add('leave_active');
                    },
                    onEnterBack: function () {
                        specialSect.classList.remove('leave_active');
                    },
                }
            })

                .set(".special_sect .specialness_cont .sect_tit_box", {
                    transition: "none",
                })

                .to(".dealy", {
                    duration: window.innerWidth <= 1240 ? '0' : '0.8',
                    ease: 'none',
                })

                .to(".special_sect .wh_bg", {
                    clipPath: 'inset(0 0 100% 0)',
                    ease: 'none',
                })
                .to(".special_sect .specialness_cont .sect_tit_box", {
                    y: window.innerWidth <= 768 ? '0vh' : '-100vh',
                    ease: 'none',
                }, "<")

                .to(".special_sect .card_wrap", {
                    clipPath: 'inset(0 0 100% 0)',
                    ease: 'none',
                }, "<");

            specialSect.__specialMoStickyResizeHandler = function () {
                if (specialSect.__specialMoStickyResizeRaf) {
                    cancelAnimationFrame(specialSect.__specialMoStickyResizeRaf);
                }
                specialSect.__specialMoStickyResizeRaf = requestAnimationFrame(function () {
                    specialSect.__specialMoStickyResizeRaf = null;

                    ScrollTrigger.refresh();
                    refreshSpecialMoStickyLayout();
                });
            };

             
            if (window.onLayoutResize) window.onLayoutResize(specialSect.__specialMoStickyResizeHandler);
            else window.addEventListener('resize', specialSect.__specialMoStickyResizeHandler);
            requestAnimationFrame(refreshSpecialMoStickyLayout);

        }

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initSpecialMoStickyScrollTrigger);
        } else {
            initSpecialMoStickyScrollTrigger();
        }
    })();

    (function () {
        function initSpecialStickyScrollTrigger() {
            if (typeof gsap === 'undefined' || typeof ScrollTrigger === 'undefined') return;

            var stickyContBox = document.querySelector('.special_sect .specialness_cont .cont_box.sticky');
            if (!stickyContBox) return;

            gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);

            var specialSect = document.querySelector('.special_sect');
            var canvasTxtWrap = document.querySelector('.special_sect .since2_wrap .canvas_txt_wrap');
            var since2ButtonBox = document.querySelector('.special_sect .since2_wrap .com_btn1_box');
            var leftTextBoxes = gsap.utils.toArray('.special_sect .since2_wrap .canvas_txt_wrap .txt_box');
            var txtType1Box = document.querySelector('.special_sect .specialness_cont .cont_box.txt_type1');
            var txtType1Parts = txtType1Box ? gsap.utils.toArray(txtType1Box.querySelectorAll('.left_tit_box .desc, .left_tit_box .tit, .left_tit_box .txt')) : [];
            var specialTextIndexes = leftTextBoxes.map(function (txtBox) {
                return parseInt(txtBox.getAttribute('data-special-index'), 10);
            }).filter(function (index) {
                return !isNaN(index);
            });
            var firstSpecialTextIndex = specialTextIndexes.length ? specialTextIndexes[0] : null;
            var lastSpecialTextIndex = specialTextIndexes.length ? specialTextIndexes[specialTextIndexes.length - 1] : null;

             
            var lastTextIndex = null;
            function setSpecialText(index) {
                if (!leftTextBoxes.length || index === null || typeof index === 'undefined') return;
                if (index === lastTextIndex) return;
                lastTextIndex = index;
                leftTextBoxes.forEach(function (txtBox) {
                    var isTarget = txtBox.getAttribute('data-special-index') === String(index);
                    txtBox.classList.toggle('is-active', isTarget);
                });
            }
            function clearSpecialText(onComplete) {
                lastTextIndex = null;
                if (!leftTextBoxes.length) {
                    if (typeof onComplete === 'function') onComplete();
                    return;
                }
                leftTextBoxes.forEach(function (txtBox) {
                    txtBox.classList.remove('is-active');
                });
                if (typeof onComplete === 'function') onComplete();
            }
            function setCanvasTxtWrapActive(active) {
                var isActive = !!active;
                var header = document.querySelector('.header');

                if (canvasTxtWrap) {
                    canvasTxtWrap.classList.toggle('active', isActive);
                }

                if (header) {
                    header.classList.remove('color_type', !isActive);
                }
            }
            function hideTxtType1() {
                if (txtType1Box) {
                    txtType1Box.classList.remove('on');
                    txtType1Box.classList.remove('hide');
                }
                if (!txtType1Parts.length) return;
                gsap.to(txtType1Parts, {
                    y: -100,
                    opacity: 0,
                    filter: 'blur(10px)',
                    duration: 0.8,
                    stagger: 0.1,
                    ease: 'power2.out',
                    overwrite: true
                });
            }
            function showTxtType1() {
                if (txtType1Box) {
                    txtType1Box.classList.remove('hide');
                    txtType1Box.classList.remove('on');
                }
                if (!txtType1Parts.length) return;
                gsap.to(txtType1Parts, {
                    y: 0,
                    opacity: 1,
                    filter: 'blur(0px)',
                    duration: 0.8,
                    stagger: 0.1,
                    ease: 'power2.out',
                    overwrite: true
                });
            }
            function setSince2ButtonActive(active) {
                if (!since2ButtonBox) return;
                since2ButtonBox.classList.toggle('active', !!active);
            }
            function setTxtType1Active(active) {
                if (!txtType1Box) return;
                txtType1Box.classList.toggle('active', !!active);
            }

             
             
             
            var rideEl = document.getElementById('spRide');
            var clipEl = document.getElementById('spRideClip');
            var mountEl = document.getElementById('spRideMount');
            var fixedCard = document.querySelector('.special_sect .first_card_list .item.fixed');
            var posterEl = fixedCard ? fixedCard.querySelector('.sp-embed__poster') : null;
            if (!rideEl || !clipEl || !mountEl || !fixedCard) return;

            var SPECIALIST_SRC = '/inc/specialist/js/specialist-3d.js?v=1790644075';
            var world = null;
            var worldLoading = null;
            function loadWorld() {
                if (worldLoading) return worldLoading;
                worldLoading = import(SPECIALIST_SRC)
                    .then(function (m) {
                        return m.createSpecialistWorld({
                            mode: 'embed',
                            mount: mountEl,
                            blurManifest: null                        });
                    })
                    .then(function (api) {
                        world = api;
                        mountEl.__spWorld = api;    
                         
                         
                         
                         
                         
                        api.setProgress(pendingProgress, true);
                         
                         
                        try { api.frame(); } catch (e) { }
                        refreshSceneBounds(api);     
                        syncCardLive();      
                        return api;
                    })
                    .catch(function (err) {
                         
                        console.error('[special_sect] 专区加载失败:', err);
                        worldLoading = null;
                        throw err;
                    });
                return worldLoading;
            }
             
            if ('IntersectionObserver' in window) {
                var spWorldIo = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
                    if (entries.some(function (e) { return e.isIntersecting; })) {
                        spWorldIo.disconnect();
                        loadWorld().catch(function () { });
                    }
                }, { rootMargin: '100% 0px' });
                spWorldIo.observe(specialSect);
            } else {
                loadWorld().catch(function () { });
            }

             
             
             
             
             
             
             
             
             
             
             
             
            (function preBootWorld() {
                try {
                    var nc = navigator.connection;
                    if (nc && nc.saveData) return;
                    if (navigator.deviceMemory && navigator.deviceMemory < 4) return;
                } catch (e) { }
                var fired = false;
                function go() {
                    if (fired) return;
                    fired = true;
                    var start = function () { loadWorld().catch(function () { }); };
                    if ('requestIdleCallback' in window) requestIdleCallback(start, { timeout: 4000 });
                    else setTimeout(start, 1500);
                }
                function afterRobot() {
                    var t0 = Date.now();
                    (function poll() {
                        if (fired) return;
                        var r = window.__robot3d;
                        if ((r && r.overlay && r.overlay.running) || Date.now() - t0 > 12000) return go();
                        setTimeout(poll, 250);
                    })();
                }
                function afterGate() {
                    var html = document.documentElement;
                    if (!html.classList.contains('intro-gate-lock')) return afterRobot();
                    var done = false;
                    var mo = new MutationObserver(function () {
                        if (!done && !html.classList.contains('intro-gate-lock')) {
                            done = true; mo.disconnect(); afterRobot();
                        }
                    });
                    mo.observe(html, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['class'] });
                     
                    setTimeout(function () {
                        if (!done) { done = true; mo.disconnect(); afterRobot(); }
                    }, 20000);
                }
                if (document.readyState === 'complete') afterGate();
                else window.addEventListener('load', afterGate, { once: true });
            })();

             
            window.addEventListener('mousemove', function (e) {
                if (world && rideActive) {
                    world.setPointer(
                        (e.clientX / window.innerWidth) * 2 - 1,
                        (e.clientY / window.innerHeight) * 2 - 1
                    );
                }
            });

             
             
             
             
            var EXPAND_PX = 412.5, SHRINK_PX = 412.5;
            var DESKTOP_RIDE_START = 0.2;
            var MOBILE_RIDE_START = 0.2;
            var EXPAND_END = 1 / 12;    
            var RIDE_END = 11 / 12;    //      〃        1 − SHRINK_PX / S
             
             
            var RIDE_P0 = 0;    
            var pendingProgress = RIDE_P0;    
             
             
             
             
             

             
            var cardRect = null;
            var cardRadius = null;
            function measureCard() {
                var r = fixedCard.getBoundingClientRect();
                cardRect = { left: r.left, top: r.top, width: r.width, height: r.height };
                if (cardRadius === null) cardRadius = parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(fixedCard).borderTopLeftRadius) || 0;
            }
            var cardStyleValues = new WeakMap();
            function setCardStyle(element, property, value) {
                var values = cardStyleValues.get(element);
                if (!values) { values = {}; cardStyleValues.set(element, values); }
                 
                var previous = values[property];
                if (previous && previous.value === value && previous.applied === element.style[property]) return;
                element.style[property] = value;
                values[property] = { value: value, applied: element.style[property] };
            }

             
             
             
             
             
             
            //
             
             
             
             
             
            //
             
             
             
             
            //
             
             
             
             
            //
             
             
            var cardAnchor = null;       
             
             
             
            function placeCard() {
                measureCard();
                 
                var a = cardAnchor || cardRect;
                setCardStyle(clipEl, 'left', cardRect.left + 'px');
                setCardStyle(clipEl, 'top', cardRect.top + 'px');
                setCardStyle(clipEl, 'width', cardRect.width + 'px');
                setCardStyle(clipEl, 'height', cardRect.height + 'px');
                setCardStyle(clipEl, 'borderRadius', cardRadius + 'px');
                setCardStyle(mountEl, 'left', -a.left + 'px');
                setCardStyle(mountEl, 'top', -a.top + 'px');
            }

             
             
             
             
            function setClip(t) {
                if (!cardRect) measureCard();
                var e = Math.min(Math.max(t, 0), 1);
                e = e * e * (3 - 2 * e);
                var L = cardRect.left * (1 - e);
                var T = cardRect.top * (1 - e);
                var W = cardRect.width + (window.innerWidth - cardRect.width) * e;
                var H = cardRect.height + (window.innerHeight - cardRect.height) * e;
                setCardStyle(clipEl, 'left', L + 'px');
                setCardStyle(clipEl, 'top', T + 'px');
                setCardStyle(clipEl, 'width', W + 'px');
                setCardStyle(clipEl, 'height', H + 'px');
                setCardStyle(clipEl, 'borderRadius', (cardRadius * (1 - e)) + 'px');
                setCardStyle(mountEl, 'left', -L + 'px');
                setCardStyle(mountEl, 'top', -T + 'px');
            }

            var rideActive = false;
             
             
             
             
             
             
             
            var cardLive = false;           
             
             
             
             
            var cardCanvasOn = false;       
            var cardLoopOn = false;         
            var sectNear = false;           
            function cardLoop() {
                if (!cardLoopOn) return;
                if (!rideActive && cardLive) {
                    placeCard();    
                }
                requestAnimationFrame(cardLoop);
            }
            function syncCardLive() {
                var want = !!world && sectNear && !rideActive && cardCanvasOn;
                if (want === cardLive) return;
                cardLive = want;
                rideEl.classList.toggle('is-cardlive', want);
                specialSect.classList.toggle('is-cardlive', want);
                if (want) {
                     
                     
                    if (pendingProgress === 0) world.setProgress(0, true);
                    world.setRunning(true);
                    if (!cardLoopOn) { cardLoopOn = true; requestAnimationFrame(cardLoop); }
                } else {
                    cardLoopOn = false;
                    if (!rideActive && world) {
                        world.setProgress(pendingProgress, true);    
                        world.frame();                               
                        world.setRunning(false);
                    }
                }
            }
             
             
             
            window.__spCardCanvas = function (on) { cardCanvasOn = !!on; syncCardLive(); };
             
            if ('IntersectionObserver' in window) {
                new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
