                    sectNear = entries.some(function (e) { return e.isIntersecting; });
                    syncCardLive();
                }, { rootMargin: '15% 0px' }).observe(specialSect);
            } else { sectNear = true; }
            function setRideActive(on) {
                if (on === rideActive) return;
                 
                 
                measureCard();
                cardAnchor = { left: cardRect.left, top: cardRect.top };
                rideActive = on;
                if (on) refreshSceneBounds();    
                rideEl.classList.toggle('is-on', on);
                if (on && cardLive) {
                     
                    cardLive = false; cardLoopOn = false;
                    rideEl.classList.remove('is-cardlive');
                    specialSect.classList.remove('is-cardlive');
                }
                 
                if (window.__robot3d && window.__robot3d.setPaused) window.__robot3d.setPaused(on);
                specialSect.classList.toggle('is-riding', on);
                 
                 
                var header = document.querySelector('.header');
                if (header) header.classList.toggle('color_type', on);
                if (world) {
                    world.setRunning(on);
                } else if (on) {
                    loadWorld().then(function (api) {
                        if (rideActive) api.setRunning(true);
                    }).catch(function () { });
                }
                if (!on) syncCardLive();    
            }

             
             
            var posterSrc0 = posterEl ? posterEl.src : '';
            function handToPoster() {
                if (!world || !posterEl) return;
                try {
                    world.frame();
                    posterEl.src = world.renderer.domElement.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.85);
                } catch (err) {   }
            }
            function restorePoster() {
                if (posterEl && posterSrc0 && posterEl.src !== posterSrc0) posterEl.src = posterSrc0;
            }

             
             
             
             
             
            var SCENE_BOUNDS = [0, 0.2910, 0.4376, 0.5777, 0.7241, 0.8751];
            var SCENE3_MID = (SCENE_BOUNDS[2] + SCENE_BOUNDS[3]) / 2;
            function refreshSceneBounds(w) {
                w = w || world;
                if (!w || !w.sceneBoundsP) return;
                try {
                    var b = w.sceneBoundsP();
                    if (b && b.length === SCENE_BOUNDS.length) {
                        SCENE_BOUNDS = b;
                        SCENE3_MID = (SCENE_BOUNDS[2] + SCENE_BOUNDS[3]) / 2;
                    }
                } catch (e) { }
            }
            function sceneTextIndex(p) {
                if (!specialTextIndexes.length) return null;
                if (p >= SCENE_BOUNDS[4]) return 6;
                if (p >= SCENE_BOUNDS[3]) return 5;
                if (p >= SCENE3_MID) return 4;
                if (p >= SCENE_BOUNDS[2]) return 3;
                if (p >= SCENE_BOUNDS[1]) return 2;
                return firstSpecialTextIndex;
            }

             
            gsap.timeline({
                scrollTrigger: {
                    trigger: stickyContBox,
                    start: 'top top',
                    end: 'top top',
                    onEnter: function () {
                        hideTxtType1();
                        setSpecialText(firstSpecialTextIndex);
                    },
                    onLeaveBack: function () {
                        clearSpecialText(showTxtType1);
                    },
                    scrub: true,
                    markers: false,
                    invalidateOnRefresh: true
                }
            });

            if (txtType1Box) {
                ScrollTrigger.create({
                    trigger: txtType1Box,
                    start: 'top top',
                    onEnter: function () {
                        setTxtType1Active(true);
                    },
                    onLeaveBack: function () {
                        setTxtType1Active(false);
                    }
                });
            }

            clearSpecialText();

            ScrollTrigger.create({
                trigger: stickyContBox,
                start: 'top top',
                end: 'bottom 150%',
                scrub: true,
                markers: false,
                invalidateOnRefresh: true,
                onRefresh: function (self) {
                    cardRect = null;
                    cardRadius = null;
                     
                     
                    var S = self.end - self.start;
                    if (S > (EXPAND_PX + SHRINK_PX) * 2) {
                        EXPAND_END = EXPAND_PX / S;
                        RIDE_END = 1 - SHRINK_PX / S;
                    } else {
                        EXPAND_END = 1 / 12; RIDE_END = 11 / 12;    
                    }
                },
                onUpdate: function (self) {
                    var rawProgress = self.progress;
                    var rideStart = window.innerWidth <= 600 ? MOBILE_RIDE_START : DESKTOP_RIDE_START;
                    var p = rideStart > 0
                        ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (rawProgress - rideStart) / (1 - rideStart)))
                        : rawProgress;
                    var inside = p > 0.0005 && p < 0.9995;
                    setRideActive(inside);
                    setCanvasTxtWrapActive(false);
                    if (!inside) {
                        if (rawProgress < rideStart) {
                            setSince2ButtonActive(false);
                            setClip(0);
                            pendingProgress = RIDE_P0;
                            if (world) world.setProgress(RIDE_P0, true);
                            clearSpecialText(showTxtType1);
                        } else if (p >= 0.9995) {
                            setSince2ButtonActive(true);
                            clearSpecialText();
                        } else {
                            setSince2ButtonActive(false);
                        }
                        return;
                    }
                    if (p < EXPAND_END) {
                         
                        setSince2ButtonActive(false);
                        setClip(p / EXPAND_END);
                        pendingProgress = RIDE_P0;
                        if (world) world.setProgress(RIDE_P0);
                        setSpecialText(firstSpecialTextIndex);
                    } else if (p < RIDE_END) {
                         
                        setSince2ButtonActive(false);
                        setCanvasTxtWrapActive(true);
                        setClip(1);
                        var rp = RIDE_P0 + (p - EXPAND_END) / (RIDE_END - EXPAND_END);
                        pendingProgress = rp;
                        if (world) world.setProgress(rp);
                        setSpecialText(sceneTextIndex(Math.min(rp, 0.9999)));    
                    } else {
                         
                         
                         
                         
                        var shrinkProgress = (p - RIDE_END) / (1 - RIDE_END);
                        setClip(1 - shrinkProgress);
                        pendingProgress = 1  ;
                        if (world) world.setProgress(1  , true);
                        setSince2ButtonActive(shrinkProgress >= 0.5);
                        clearSpecialText();
                    }
                },
                onEnter: function () {
                    cardRect = null;
                    restorePoster();
                    setSince2ButtonActive(false);
                },
                onEnterBack: function () {
                    cardRect = null;
                    setSince2ButtonActive(false);
                },
                onLeave: function () {
                     
                     
                    if (world) world.setProgress(1  , true);
                    handToPoster();
                    cardCanvasOn = true;    
                    setRideActive(false);
                    syncCardLive();         
                    setSince2ButtonActive(true);
                    clearSpecialText();
                },
                onLeaveBack: function () {
                     
                     
                    restorePoster();
                    setRideActive(false);
                    syncCardLive();
                    setSince2ButtonActive(false);
                    clearSpecialText(showTxtType1);
                }
            });
        }

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initSpecialStickyScrollTrigger);
        } else {
            initSpecialStickyScrollTrigger();
        }
    })();

    (function () {
        function initReviewSwiper() {
            var reviewSwiperEl = document.querySelector('.review_sect .since3_cont .review-swiper');
            var reviewPaginationEl = document.querySelector('.review_sect .since3_cont .review_progress');
            if (!reviewSwiperEl) return;

            function enableReviewSwiper() {
                if (window.reviewSince3Swiper || typeof Swiper === 'undefined') return;

                window.reviewSince3Swiper = new Swiper(reviewSwiperEl, {
                    slidesPerView: 'auto',
                    spaceBetween: 24,
                    speed: 600,
                    grabCursor: true,
                    watchOverflow: true,
                    breakpoints: {
                        2240: {
                            spaceBetween: 30
                        },
                        1640: {
                            spaceBetween: 20
                        },
                        600: {
                            spaceBetween: 15
                        },
                        0: {
                            spaceBetween: 10
                        },
                    },

                    pagination: reviewPaginationEl ? {
                        el: reviewPaginationEl,
                        type: 'progressbar'
                    } : undefined
                });
            }

            function disableReviewSwiper() {
                if (!window.reviewSince3Swiper) return;

                window.reviewSince3Swiper.destroy(true, true);
                window.reviewSince3Swiper = null;
            }

            function syncReviewSwiper() {
                if (window.innerWidth <= 1640) {
                    enableReviewSwiper();
                } else {
                    disableReviewSwiper();
                }

                if (window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh) {
                    window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh([0, 120, 360]);
                }
            }

            syncReviewSwiper();

            if (!window.reviewSince3SwiperResizeBound) {
                window.reviewSince3SwiperResizeBound = true;
                if (window.onLayoutResize) window.onLayoutResize(syncReviewSwiper);
                else window.addEventListener('resize', syncReviewSwiper);
            }

            if (window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh) {
                window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh([0, 120, 360]);
            }
        }

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initReviewSwiper);
        } else {
            initReviewSwiper();
        }
    })();




    (function () {
        function initPickSectWrapDefaultScrollTrigger() {
            if (typeof gsap === 'undefined' || typeof ScrollTrigger === 'undefined') return;

            var pickSectWrap = document.querySelector('.pick_sect_wrap');
            var pickSectTitBox = pickSectWrap ? pickSectWrap.querySelector('.pickup_sect .sect_tit_box') : null;
            var pickSectImgBox = pickSectWrap ? pickSectWrap.querySelector('.pickup_sect .img_box') : null;
            var pickupTimelineVideo = pickSectWrap ? pickSectWrap.querySelector('.pickup_sect .img .item') : null;
            var characterPortfolioVideoStartTime = 0;
            var characterStickerState = null;
            var pickupTimelineVideoInView = false;
            var pickupTimelineVideoReady = false;
            var pickupTimelineVideoDelayTimer = null;
            var pickupTimelineVideoLoadHandler = null;
            var characterStickerStartTime = 0.9;
            if (!pickSectWrap || pickSectWrap.pickSectWrapScrollTriggerReady) return;
            pickSectWrap.pickSectWrapScrollTriggerReady = true;

             
             
            var isDesktopCharacterTimelineMode = window.innerWidth > 1040 && typeof gsap !== 'undefined' && typeof ScrollTrigger !== 'undefined';
            var characterPortfolioVideo = document.querySelector('.character_sect .portfolio_character .item');

             
             
             
             
             
            function createScrollVideoScrubber(video) {
                var duration = 0;
                var isSeeking = false;
                var pendingTime = null;
                var loadRequested = false;
                var scrubber = { progress: 0 };

                if (!video) return null;

                function readDuration() {
                    if (isFinite(video.duration) && video.duration > 0) duration = video.duration;
                }
                readDuration();
                video.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', readDuration);
                video.addEventListener('durationchange', readDuration);
                video.addEventListener('seeked', function () {
                    isSeeking = false;
                    if (pendingTime !== null) {
                        var next = pendingTime;
                        pendingTime = null;
                        seekTo(next);
                    }
                });

                function seekTo(time) {
                    if (!duration) return;
                    time = Math.min(Math.max(time, 0), Math.max(duration - 0.04, 0));
                    if (Math.abs(video.currentTime - time) < 1 / 48) return;
                    if (isSeeking) {
                        pendingTime = time;
                        return;
                    }
                    isSeeking = true;
                    try {
                        video.currentTime = time;
                    } catch (e) {
                        isSeeking = false;
                    }
                }

                scrubber.ensureLoaded = function () {
                    if (loadRequested) return;
                    loadRequested = true;
                    video.muted = true;
                    video.playsInline = true;
                    video.loop = false;
                    video.removeAttribute('autoplay');
                    video.preload = 'auto';
                    video.pause();
                    video.load();
                };
                scrubber.apply = function () {
                    if (!video.paused) video.pause();
                    scrubber.ensureLoaded();
                    seekTo(scrubber.progress * duration);
                };
                scrubber.video = video;
                return scrubber;
            }

             
            var pickupVideoScrubber = null;
            var characterVideoScrubber = null;
             
             
            window.portfolioCharacterVideoScrubActive = false;
            window.portfolioCharacterVideoTimelineMode = !!isDesktopCharacterTimelineMode;

            function syncCharacterStickerState(active) {
                if (characterStickerState === active) return;
                characterStickerState = active;

                var characterTitle = document.querySelector('.character_sect .sect_tit_box');
                var characterCont = document.querySelector('.character_sect .character_cont');
                var stickerRoot = document.querySelector('.character_wrap[data-area="sticker"]');

                if (characterTitle) {
                    characterTitle.classList.toggle('active', active);
                }

                if (characterCont) {
                    characterCont.classList.toggle('active', active);
                }

                if (window.portfolioStickerBoard && typeof window.portfolioStickerBoard.setEnabled === 'function') {
                    window.portfolioStickerBoard.setEnabled(active, stickerRoot);
                }

                if (!active && window.portfolioStickerBoard && typeof window.portfolioStickerBoard.clear === 'function' && stickerRoot) {
                    window.portfolioStickerBoard.clear(stickerRoot);
                }

                 
                 
                if (!active && !isDesktopCharacterTimelineMode && window.portfolioCharacterVideoController && typeof window.portfolioCharacterVideoController.reset === 'function') {
                    window.portfolioCharacterVideoController.reset();
                }
            }

            function observeCompactCharacterStickerState() {
                var characterSect = document.querySelector('.character_sect');
                var characterCont = document.querySelector('.character_sect .character_cont');
                var target = characterCont || characterSect;
                if (!target) return;

                if (!('IntersectionObserver' in window)) {
                    syncCharacterStickerState(true);
                    return;
                }

                new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
                    entries.forEach(function (entry) {
                        syncCharacterStickerState(entry.isIntersecting);
                    });
                }, {
                    threshold: 0.25
                }).observe(target);
            }

            function playPickupTimelineVideo() {
                if (!pickupTimelineVideo) return;
                if (!pickupTimelineVideo.paused) return;

                pickupTimelineVideo.muted = true;
                pickupTimelineVideo.loop = true;
                pickupTimelineVideo.playsInline = true;
                pickupTimelineVideo.preload = 'auto';

                var playPromise = pickupTimelineVideo.play();
                if (playPromise && playPromise.catch) {
                    playPromise.catch(function () { });
                }
            }

            function pausePickupTimelineVideo(resetTime) {
                if (!pickupTimelineVideo) return;

                pickupTimelineVideo.pause();
                if (resetTime) {
                    try {
                        pickupTimelineVideo.currentTime = 0;
                    } catch (e) { }
                }
            }

            function updatePickupTimelineVideoPlayback() {
                if (!pickupTimelineVideo) return;

                if (
                    pickupTimelineVideoInView &&
                    pickupTimelineVideoReady &&
                    !document.hidden
                ) {
                    playPickupTimelineVideo();
                    return;
                }

                pausePickupTimelineVideo(!pickupTimelineVideoInView);
            }

            function queuePickupTimelineVideoPlay() {
                clearTimeout(pickupTimelineVideoDelayTimer);
                pickupTimelineVideoReady = false;
                if (pickupTimelineVideoLoadHandler) {
                    pickupTimelineVideo.removeEventListener('loadeddata', pickupTimelineVideoLoadHandler);
                    pickupTimelineVideoLoadHandler = null;
                }

                pickupTimelineVideo.preload = 'auto';
                pausePickupTimelineVideo(true);

                function startDelayAfterFirstFrame() {
                    clearTimeout(pickupTimelineVideoDelayTimer);
                    pickupTimelineVideoDelayTimer = setTimeout(function () {
                        pickupTimelineVideoDelayTimer = null;
                        pickupTimelineVideoReady = true;
                        updatePickupTimelineVideoPlayback();
                    }, 1000);
                }

                if (pickupTimelineVideo.readyState >= 2) {
                    startDelayAfterFirstFrame();
                    return;
                }

                pickupTimelineVideoLoadHandler = function () {
                    pickupTimelineVideo.removeEventListener('loadeddata', pickupTimelineVideoLoadHandler);
                    pickupTimelineVideoLoadHandler = null;
                    startDelayAfterFirstFrame();
                };
                pickupTimelineVideo.addEventListener('loadeddata', pickupTimelineVideoLoadHandler, { once: true });
                pickupTimelineVideo.load();
            }

            function resetPickupTimelineVideoObserverState() {
                clearTimeout(pickupTimelineVideoDelayTimer);
                pickupTimelineVideoDelayTimer = null;
                if (pickupTimelineVideoLoadHandler) {
                    pickupTimelineVideo.removeEventListener('loadeddata', pickupTimelineVideoLoadHandler);
                    pickupTimelineVideoLoadHandler = null;
                }
                pickupTimelineVideoReady = false;
                pausePickupTimelineVideo(true);
            }

            if (isDesktopCharacterTimelineMode && characterPortfolioVideo && 'IntersectionObserver' in window) {
                 
                var pickSectPreloadObserver = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
                    entries.forEach(function (entry) {
                        if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
                        if (window.portfolioCharacterVideoController && typeof window.portfolioCharacterVideoController.prime === 'function') {
                            window.portfolioCharacterVideoController.prime();
                        }
                        pickSectPreloadObserver.disconnect();
                    });
                }, { rootMargin: '100% 0px' });
                pickSectPreloadObserver.observe(pickSectWrap);
            }

            if (pickupTimelineVideo) {
                pausePickupTimelineVideo(true);

                if ('IntersectionObserver' in window && pickSectImgBox) {
                    new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
                        entries.forEach(function (entry) {
                            pickupTimelineVideoInView = entry.isIntersecting;
                            if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                                queuePickupTimelineVideoPlay();
                            } else {
                                resetPickupTimelineVideoObserverState();
                            }
                        });
                    }, {
                        threshold: 0.3
                    }).observe(pickSectImgBox);
                } else {
                    pickupTimelineVideoInView = true;
                    queuePickupTimelineVideoPlay();
                }

                document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () {
                    if (document.hidden) {
                        pausePickupTimelineVideo(false);
                    } else {
                        updatePickupTimelineVideoPlayback();
                    }
                });
            }

            if (window.innerWidth <= 1040) {
                window.portfolioCharacterVideoTimelineMode = false;
                syncCharacterStickerState(false);
                observeCompactCharacterStickerState();
                return;
            }

            gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);

            var pickSectTimeline = gsap.timeline({
                scrollTrigger: {
                    trigger: pickSectWrap,
                    start: 'top top',
                    end: 'bottom 100%',
                    // markers: true,
                    invalidateOnRefresh: true,
                    scrub: true,
                    onEnterBack: function () {
                        syncCharacterStickerState(pickSectTimeline.time() >= characterStickerStartTime);
                    },
                    onLeave: function () {
                        if (window.portfolioCharacterVideoController && typeof window.portfolioCharacterVideoController.reset === 'function') {
                            window.portfolioCharacterVideoController.reset();
                        }
                        characterVideoPlaybackStarted = false;
                    },
                    onLeaveBack: function () {
                        syncCharacterStickerState(false);
                        if (window.portfolioCharacterVideoController && typeof window.portfolioCharacterVideoController.reset === 'function') {
                            window.portfolioCharacterVideoController.reset();
                        }
                        characterVideoPlaybackStarted = false;
                    }
                }
            });

            pickSectTimeline.set('.pick_sect_wrap .sect_tit_box p > span', {
                transition: 'unset'
            })

            pickSectTimeline.set('.pickup_sect .img', {
                transition: 'unset'
            })

            pickSectTimeline.set('.character_sect .portfolio_character .img', {
                transition: 'unset'
            })

             
             
             
             
             
            var characterVideoPlaybackStarted = false;

            pickSectTimeline
                .to({}, {
                    duration: 0.3,
                    ease: 'none',
                })
                 
                .addLabel('pickSectTransition')
                .to('.pickup_sect .sect_tit_box .sm_tit p > span', {
                    y: "100%",
                    duration: 0.1,
                    ease: 'none',
                })
                .to('.pickup_sect .sect_tit_box .desc p > span', {
                    y: "100%",
                    duration: 0.1,
                    ease: 'none',
                }, ">-0.05")
                .to('.pickup_sect .sect_tit_box .big_tit  p > span', {
                    y: "100%",
                    duration: 0.1,
                    ease: 'none',
                }, ">-0.05")

            pickSectTimeline.to('.pickup_sect .img', {
                y: "100%",
                opacity: 0,
                duration: 0.3,
            }, "<");

             
             
             
             
            pickSectTimeline
                .fromTo('.character_sect .sect_tit_box .sm_tit p > span', { y: "100%" }, {
                    y: "0%",
                    duration: 0.1,
                    ease: 'none',
                    immediateRender: true
                }, "<")
                .fromTo('.character_sect .sect_tit_box .desc p > span', { y: "100%" }, {
                    y: "0%",
                    duration: 0.1,
                    ease: 'none',
                    immediateRender: true
                }, ">-0.05")
                .fromTo('.character_sect .sect_tit_box .big_tit  p > span', { y: "100%" }, {
                    y: "0%",
                    duration: 0.1,
                    ease: 'none',
                    immediateRender: true
                }, ">-0.05")
                .to('.character_sect .sect_tit_box .reset_btn', {
                    opacity: "1",
                    duration: 0.1,
                    ease: 'none',
                }, "<")
                .to('.character_sect .portfolio_character .img', {
                    x: "0",
                    y: "20%",
                    scale: 1,
                    duration: 0.3,
                }, "<")
                .addLabel('characterVideoScrub')
                .to('.delay', {
                    duration: 0.3,
                })

             
             
            characterStickerStartTime = pickSectTimeline.labels.pickSectTransition + 0.1;
            characterPortfolioVideoStartTime = pickSectTimeline.labels.characterVideoScrub;

            var characterCont = document.querySelector('.character_sect .character_cont');
            var characterTitleBoxes = document.querySelectorAll('.character_sect .character_wrap .sect_tit_box');
            var pickupTitleSpans = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('.pickup_sect .sect_tit_box p > span'));
            pickSectTimeline.eventCallback('onUpdate', function () {
                if (this.time() < characterStickerStartTime && characterCont) {
                    if (characterCont.classList.contains('active')) characterCont.classList.remove('active');
                    characterTitleBoxes.forEach(function (box) {
                        if (box.classList.contains('active')) box.classList.remove('active');
                    });
                }
                 
                 
                 
                if (this.time() < this.labels.pickSectTransition) {
                    gsap.set(pickupTitleSpans, { clearProps: 'transform' });
                }
                if (this.time() >= characterStickerStartTime) {
                    syncCharacterStickerState(true);
                }

                 
                if (window.portfolioCharacterVideoController) {
                    if (this.time() >= characterPortfolioVideoStartTime) {
                        if (!characterVideoPlaybackStarted && typeof window.portfolioCharacterVideoController.playOnceIfReady === 'function') {
                            characterVideoPlaybackStarted = !!window.portfolioCharacterVideoController.playOnceIfReady();
                        } else if (!isDesktopCharacterTimelineMode && typeof window.portfolioCharacterVideoController.playIfReady === 'function') {
                            window.portfolioCharacterVideoController.playIfReady();
                        }
                    } else {
                        if (characterVideoPlaybackStarted && typeof window.portfolioCharacterVideoController.reset === 'function') {
                            window.portfolioCharacterVideoController.reset();
                        } else if (!isDesktopCharacterTimelineMode && typeof window.portfolioCharacterVideoController.prime === 'function') {
                            window.portfolioCharacterVideoController.prime();
                        }
                        characterVideoPlaybackStarted = false;
                    }
                }
            });





        }

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initPickSectWrapDefaultScrollTrigger);
        } else {
            initPickSectWrapDefaultScrollTrigger();
        }
    })();




    (function () {
        function initReviewDefaultScrollTrigger() {
            var reviewSect = document.querySelector('.review_sect_default[data-review-scroll-type="default"]');
            if (!reviewSect || reviewSect.reviewDefaultScrollTriggerReady) return;

            if (window.innerWidth <= 1040) {
                var since1Cont = reviewSect.querySelector('.since1_cont');

                    since1Cont.classList.remove('hide');
                    return;

                var since1Observer = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
                    entries.forEach(function (entry) {
                        if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                            since1Cont.classList.add('active');
                            since1Cont.classList.remove('hide');
                            since1Observer.disconnect();
                        }
                    });
                }, {
                    threshold: 0.9
                });
                since1Observer.observe(since1Cont);
                return;
            }

            var REVIEW_DOT_CENTER_ROTATE = 720;
            var REVIEW_DOT_BOX_GROW_ROTATE = 180;
             
             
             
            var REVIEW_DOT_BOX_SHRINK_ROTATE = 180;
            var REVIEW_DOT_BOX_SHRINK_BOX_END = Math.ceil(REVIEW_DOT_BOX_GROW_ROTATE / 360) * 360;
            var REVIEW_DOT_SHRINK_DOT_END = REVIEW_DOT_CENTER_ROTATE + REVIEW_DOT_BOX_SHRINK_ROTATE - (REVIEW_DOT_BOX_SHRINK_BOX_END - REVIEW_DOT_BOX_GROW_ROTATE);
            if (REVIEW_DOT_SHRINK_DOT_END % 360 !== 0) {
                console.warn('[review] 旋转常量不满足条件: 收缩终点角度必须是 360 的倍数(SHRINK_ROTATE − (SHRINK_BOX_END − GROW) ≡ 0 mod 360). 见上方注释.');
            }
             
             
            var REVIEW_DOT_BOX_COVER_ROTATE = 135;
             
             
             
             
            var REVIEW_DOT_BOX_GROW_SCALE = 22;
             
             
             
             
             
             
             
             
            function getReviewDotCoverScale() {
                var dotEl = document.querySelector(reviewDot);
                var dotW = dotEl ? dotEl.offsetWidth : 0;
                if (!dotW) return REVIEW_DOT_BOX_GROW_SCALE;
                return Math.hypot(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight) / (0.6082 * dotW) * 1.01;
            }
             
             
            var REVIEW_DOT_BOX_GROW_START = 1.644;
            var REVIEW_DOT_BOX_GROW_DURATION = 0.66;
             
            var REVIEW_DOT_BOX_GROW_SCALE_DURATION = REVIEW_DOT_BOX_GROW_DURATION * REVIEW_DOT_BOX_COVER_ROTATE / REVIEW_DOT_BOX_GROW_ROTATE;
             
             
             
             
             
             
            var REVIEW_DOT_BOX_SHRINK_START = 0.15;
            var REVIEW_DOT_BOX_SHRINK_DURATION = 2.5;
             
            var REVIEW_DOT_BOX_SHRINK_SCALE_DURATION = REVIEW_DOT_BOX_SHRINK_DURATION * REVIEW_DOT_BOX_COVER_ROTATE / REVIEW_DOT_BOX_GROW_ROTATE;
            var REVIEW_DOT_BOX_SHRINK_SCALE_START = REVIEW_DOT_BOX_SHRINK_START + REVIEW_DOT_BOX_SHRINK_DURATION - REVIEW_DOT_BOX_SHRINK_SCALE_DURATION;
             
             
             
             
             
             
             
             
             
             
            var REVIEW_DOT_LAND_DURATION = 1.1;
            var REVIEW_DOT_LAND_START = REVIEW_DOT_BOX_SHRINK_START + REVIEW_DOT_BOX_SHRINK_DURATION;
            var REVIEW_DOT_LAND_END = REVIEW_DOT_LAND_START + REVIEW_DOT_LAND_DURATION;
             
            var REVIEW_DOT_LAND_RISE_RATIO = 0.5;
             
             
             
             
             
             
            var REVIEW_DOT_LAND_ROTATE = -720;
            if ((REVIEW_DOT_SHRINK_DOT_END + REVIEW_DOT_LAND_ROTATE) % 360 !== 0) {
                console.warn('[review] 旋转常量不满足条件: 飞行终点角度(SHRINK_DOT_END + LAND_ROTATE = '
                    + (REVIEW_DOT_SHRINK_DOT_END + REVIEW_DOT_LAND_ROTATE) + ') 必须是 360 的倍数. 否则 .fliter 重叠补丁会落在对侧. 见上方注释.');
            }
             
             
             
             
             
             
            var REVIEW_LOGO_LAND_DURATION = 0.46;
            var REVIEW_LOGO_LAND_LEAD = 0.2;
            var REVIEW_LOGO_LAND_START = REVIEW_DOT_LAND_START - REVIEW_LOGO_LAND_LEAD - REVIEW_LOGO_LAND_DURATION;
             
            var REVIEW_SINCE4_END_HOLD = 1.0;
            function restoreReviewSince1EndState() {
                gsap.set(reviewDot, { x: "-50%", y: "50%", rotate: REVIEW_DOT_CENTER_ROTATE, left: "50%", bottom: "50%", scale: 1 });

            function settleReviewSince1AtEnd() {
                if (!reviewSince1Timeline) return;
                var since1Trigger = reviewSince1Timeline.scrollTrigger;
                var since1Scrub = since1Trigger && typeof since1Trigger.getTween === 'function' ? since1Trigger.getTween() : null;

            }

            var REVIEW_SINCE1_CATCH_TIME = 0.4;
            var reviewSince1IntroOn = false;
            function showReviewSince1Intro() {
                if (reviewSince1IntroOn) return;

            var REVIEW_DOT_CATCH_DURATION = 0.45;
            var REVIEW_DOT_CATCH_HOLD_MS = 250;       
            var REVIEW_DOT_CATCH_COOLDOWN_MS = 600;   
            function makeReviewScrollCatch(options) {
                var holdMs = options && typeof options.holdMs === 'number' ? options.holdMs : REVIEW_DOT_CATCH_HOLD_MS;
                 
                 
                var upOffset = options && typeof options.upOffset === 'number' ? options.upOffset : 0;
                 
                 
                var downOffset = options && typeof options.downOffset === 'number' ? options.downOffset : 0;
                 
                var downOnly = !!(options && options.downOnly);
                var side = null;                      
                var busy = false;
                var cooldownUntil = 0;
                var proxy = { y: 0 };
                function sideOf(y, lineY, fallback) {
                    if (y > lineY + 2) return 'below';
                    if (y < lineY - 2) return 'above';
                    return fallback;
                }
                function check(lineY) {
                    if (busy) return;
                    var y = window.scrollY;
                    var nextSide = sideOf(y, lineY, side);
                    if (side === null) {
                        side = nextSide;
                        return;
                    }
                     
                     
                    if (window.isScrollJumping) {
                        side = nextSide;
                        return;
                    }
                    if (nextSide === side) return;
                    side = nextSide;
                    if (downOnly && nextSide === 'above') return;
                    if (performance.now() < cooldownUntil) return;

                    busy = true;
                     
                    var targetY = nextSide === 'above' ? lineY - upOffset : lineY + downOffset;
                    var smooth = typeof lenis !== 'undefined' ? lenis : null;
                    var finished = false;
                    var finish = function () {
                        if (finished) return;
                        finished = true;
                         
                         
                         
                         
                         
                         
                         
                         
                        var landed = !window.isScrollJumping && Math.abs(window.scrollY - targetY) <= 2;
                         
                        if (landed && smooth && typeof smooth.stop === 'function') smooth.stop();
                        setTimeout(function () {
                            if (smooth && landed) {
                                 
                                if (window.isScrollJumping) {
                                     
                                     
                                    smooth.isStopped = false;
                                } else if (typeof smooth.start === 'function') {
                                    smooth.start();
                                }
                            }
                             
                             
                             
                             
                             
                             
                             
                            side = sideOf(window.scrollY, lineY, nextSide);
                            cooldownUntil = performance.now() + REVIEW_DOT_CATCH_COOLDOWN_MS;
                            busy = false;
                             
                        }, landed ? holdMs : 0);
                    };
                     
                     
                    setTimeout(finish, REVIEW_DOT_CATCH_DURATION * 1000 + 400);
                    if (smooth && typeof smooth.scrollTo === 'function') {
                        smooth.scrollTo(targetY, { duration: REVIEW_DOT_CATCH_DURATION, easing: function (t) { return 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3); }, lock: true, force: true, onComplete: finish });
                        return;
                    }
                     
                     
                    proxy.y = y;
                    gsap.to(proxy, {
                        y: targetY,
                        duration: REVIEW_DOT_CATCH_DURATION,
                        ease: 'power3.out',
                        overwrite: true,
                        onUpdate: function () { window.scrollTo(0, proxy.y); },
                        onComplete: finish
                    });
                }
                return { check: check };
            }
            var reviewDotCatch = makeReviewScrollCatch({ holdMs: REVIEW_DOT_CATCH_HOLD_MS });
             
            function getReviewDotCatchLineY(trigger) {
                var unit = (trigger.end - trigger.start) / reviewSince1Timeline.duration();
                return trigger.start + REVIEW_SINCE1_CATCH_TIME * unit;
            }
            function reviewDotCatchCheck(trigger) {
                if (!reviewSince1Timeline) return;
                reviewDotCatch.check(getReviewDotCatchLineY(trigger));
            }
             
             
             
            var reviewSectForNav = document.querySelector('.review_sect');
            if (reviewSectForNav) {
                reviewSectForNav.siteNavScrollY = function () {
                    var trigger = reviewSince1Timeline && reviewSince1Timeline.scrollTrigger;
                    if (!trigger) return null;
                    return getReviewDotCatchLineY(trigger) + 4;
                };
            }

             
             
             
             
            var REVIEW_SINCE3_CATCH_HOLD_MS = 800;
             
             
             
            var reviewSince3Catch = makeReviewScrollCatch({ holdMs: REVIEW_SINCE3_CATCH_HOLD_MS, downOnly: true, downOffset: 3, upOffset: 3 });
            var reviewSince3LineTrigger = ScrollTrigger.create({

                }, 'since4DotScale+=' + REVIEW_DOT_LAND_START)
                 
                 
                .fromTo(reviewDot, {
                    x: "-50%",
                    y: "-100%",
                    left: "50%",
                    bottom: "50%",
                    width: "34.5%",
                    scale: 1
                }, {

            // .to('.review_sect .since4_cont .tit_box .tit', { 'gap': "2em" }, "<")




             
             
             
            var logoProxy = { p: 0 };
            var logoUnits = null;
            var logoBoxEl = document.querySelector('.review_sect .since1_cont .logo_box');
            var logoBoxEls2 = document.querySelectorAll('.review_sect .since1_cont .logo_box2');
             
             

            function measureLogoUnits() {
                var boxRect = null;
                if (logoBoxEl) {
                     
                     
                    var savedTransform = logoBoxEl.style.getPropertyValue('transform');
                    var savedPriority = logoBoxEl.style.getPropertyPriority('transform');
                    try {
                        logoBoxEl.style.setProperty('transform', 'none');
                        boxRect = logoBoxEl.getBoundingClientRect();
                    } finally {
                        if (savedTransform) {
                            logoBoxEl.style.setProperty('transform', savedTransform, savedPriority);
                        } else {
                            logoBoxEl.style.removeProperty('transform');
                        }
                    }
                }
                logoUnits = {
                    rem: parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).fontSize) || 10,
                    vw: window.innerWidth,
                    vh: window.innerHeight,
                    startWidth: boxRect && boxRect.width ? boxRect.width : 0,
                    startHeight: boxRect && boxRect.height ? boxRect.height : 0
                };
            }

            function getLogoProxyTransform(p) {
                if (!logoUnits) measureLogoUnits();
                var rem = logoUnits.rem;
                var W0 = logoUnits.startWidth || (70 * rem);
                var H0 = logoUnits.startHeight || (W0 * 0.5788);
                var Wp = W0 + (18 * rem - W0) * p;
                var X = (logoUnits.vw / 2) + (15 * rem - logoUnits.vw / 2) * p
                    - 0.5 * (1 - p) * Wp - logoUnits.vw / 2;
                var Y = (logoUnits.vh / 2) + (10 * rem - logoUnits.vh / 2) * p
                    - 0.5 * (1 - p) * H0 - logoUnits.vh / 2;
                return 'translate3d(' + X + 'px,' + Y + 'px,0) scale(' + (Wp / W0) + ')';
            }

            function syncLogoBox2StartTransform() {
                if (!logoBoxEls2 || !logoBoxEls2.length) return;
                var transformValue = getLogoProxyTransform(1);
                Array.prototype.forEach.call(logoBoxEls2, function (logoBoxEl2) {
                    logoBoxEl2.style.transformOrigin = '0 0';
                    logoBoxEl2.style.transform = transformValue;
                });
            }

            function applyLogoProxy() {
                if (!logoBoxEl) return;
                logoBoxEl.style.transform = getLogoProxyTransform(logoProxy.p);
                return;
                if (!logoUnits) measureLogoUnits();
                var rem = logoUnits.rem;
                var p = logoProxy.p;
                var W0 = logoUnits.startWidth || (70 * rem);
                var H0 = logoUnits.startHeight || (W0 * 0.5788);    
                var Wp = W0 + (18 * rem - W0) * p;           
                 
                var X = (logoUnits.vw / 2) + (15 * rem - logoUnits.vw / 2) * p
                    - 0.5 * (1 - p) * Wp - logoUnits.vw / 2;
                 
                var Y = (logoUnits.vh / 2) + (10 * rem - logoUnits.vh / 2) * p
                    - 0.5 * (1 - p) * H0 - logoUnits.vh / 2;
                var transformValue = 'translate3d(' + X + 'px,' + Y + 'px,0) scale(' + (Wp / W0) + ')';
                if (logoBoxEl) {
                    logoBoxEl.style.transform = transformValue;
                }
            }

            function resetLogoProxyInline() {
                if (logoBoxEl) {
                    gsap.set(logoBoxEl, { clearProps: 'transform,transformOrigin' });
                }
            }

            function primeLogoProxyInline() {
                if (logoBoxEl) {
                    logoBoxEl.style.transformOrigin = '0 0';    
                }
                applyLogoProxy();
            }

            syncLogoBox2StartTransform();

            if (logoBoxEl) {
                ScrollTrigger.addEventListener('refresh', function () {
                    measureLogoUnits();
                    syncLogoBox2StartTransform();
                    if (reviewSince1Timeline && reviewSince1Timeline.scrollTrigger && reviewSince1Timeline.scrollTrigger.progress > 0) {
                        primeLogoProxyInline();
                    } else {
                        resetLogoProxyInline();
                    }
                });
            }

            // var reviewSince1Timeline = gsap.timeline({
            //     scrollTrigger: {
            //         trigger: ".review_sect .since1_cont",
            //         start: 'top top',
            //         end: 'bottom+=200% top',
            //         markers: false,
            //         scrub: 0.35,
            //         snap: {
            //             snapTo: [0, 1],
            //             delay: 0.05,
            //             duration: {
            //                 min: 0.2,
            //                 max: 0.45
            //             },
            //             ease: "power1.inOut"
            //         },
            //         invalidateOnRefresh: true,
            //         onEnter: function () {
            //             $('.review_sect .since1_cont').addClass('active');
            //             $('.review_sect .since1_cont').removeClass('hide');
            //             gsap.set(reviewDot, { opacity: 1, overwrite: true });
            //         },
            //         onEnterBack: function () {
            //             $('.review_sect .since1_cont').addClass('active');
            //             $('.review_sect .since1_cont').removeClass('hide');
            //             gsap.set(reviewDot, { opacity: 1, overwrite: true });
            //         },
            //         onLeaveBack: function () {
            //             // $('.review_sect .since1_cont').addClass('hide');

            //             // setTimeout(function () {
            //             // $('.review_sect .since1_cont').removeClass('active');
            //             // }, 400);

            //         }
            //     }
            // });


            var reviewSince1Timeline = gsap.timeline({
                scrollTrigger: {
                    trigger: ".review_sect .since1_cont",
                    start: 'top top',
                     
                     
                     
                     
                     
                    endTrigger: ".review_sect .since3_cont",
                    end: 'top top',
                    // markers: true,
                    scrub: 1,
                    invalidateOnRefresh: true,
                    onEnter: function () {
                        showReviewSince1Intro();
                        primeLogoProxyInline();
                    },
                    onEnterBack: function () {
                        showReviewSince1Intro();
                        primeLogoProxyInline();
                    },
                    onUpdate: function (self) {
                        var t = self.progress * reviewSince1Timeline.duration();
                        if (self.direction < 0 && t < REVIEW_SINCE1_INTRO_HIDE_TIME) {
                            hideReviewSince1Intro();
                        } else if (self.direction > 0 && t >= REVIEW_SINCE1_INTRO_SHOW_TIME) {
                            showReviewSince1Intro();
                        }
                        reviewDotCatchCheck(self);
                    },
                    onLeave: function () {
                         
                         
                        settleReviewSince1AtEnd();
                    },
                    onLeaveBack: function () {
                         
                         
                         
                        var since1Trigger = reviewSince1Timeline && reviewSince1Timeline.scrollTrigger;
                        var since1Scrub = since1Trigger && typeof since1Trigger.getTween === 'function' ? since1Trigger.getTween() : null;
                         
                        if (since1Scrub) since1Scrub.progress(1);
                        hideReviewSince1Intro();
                        logoProxy.p = 0;
                        resetLogoProxyInline();
                    }
                }
            });

             
             
             
             
             
             
             
            var reviewSince2BigTitleSpans = '.review_sect .since2_cont .since2_tit_box .tit > span';
            gsap.set(reviewSince2BigTitleSpans, { transition: 'none', y: 0, yPercent: 100 });

            reviewSince1Timeline
                .to('.dleay', { duration: 0.4 })
                .to('.review_sect .since1_cont .dot_box .fliter', {
                    opacity: 0,
                    duration: 0.1,
                    ease: "none",
                })
                .to(logoProxy, {
                    p: 1,
                    duration: 0.35,
                    ease: "none",
                    onUpdate: applyLogoProxy
                })
                 
                 
                .to(reviewDot, {
                    keyframes: [
                        {
                            left: "50%",
                            bottom: "50%",
                            x: "-50%",
                            y: "-100%",
                            rotate: REVIEW_DOT_CENTER_ROTATE / 2,
                            scale: 1,
                            duration: 0.35,
                            ease: "none",
                        },
                        {
                            y: "50%",
                             
                            rotate: REVIEW_DOT_CENTER_ROTATE,
                            duration: 0.35,
                            // ease: "expo.out",
                        }
                    ]
                }, "<")
                 
                 
                 
                 
                 
                 
                 
                 
                 
                 
                 
                 
                .fromTo(reviewSince2BigTitleSpans, { yPercent: 100 }, {
                    yPercent: 0,
                    duration: 0.4,
                    stagger: 0.12,
                    ease: "power3.out",
                    immediateRender: false
                }, 0.8)
                 
                 
                 
                .fromTo(reviewDotBox, { '--dot-box-scale': 1 }, {
                    '--dot-box-scale': getReviewDotCoverScale,
                     
                    duration: REVIEW_DOT_BOX_GROW_SCALE_DURATION,
                    ease: "power2.inOut",
                    immediateRender: false
                }, REVIEW_DOT_BOX_GROW_START)
                .fromTo(reviewDotBox, { '--dot-box-rotate': '0deg' }, {
                    '--dot-box-rotate': REVIEW_DOT_BOX_GROW_ROTATE + 'deg',
                    duration: REVIEW_DOT_BOX_GROW_DURATION,
                    ease: "none",
                    immediateRender: false
                }, REVIEW_DOT_BOX_GROW_START)
                .fromTo(reviewSince2TitleBox, { opacity: 0 }, {
                    opacity: 1,
                    duration: 0.16,
                    ease: "none",
                    immediateRender: false
                }, REVIEW_DOT_BOX_GROW_START + REVIEW_DOT_BOX_GROW_DURATION - 0.16)

        }

        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initReviewDefaultScrollTrigger);
        } else {
            initReviewDefaultScrollTrigger();
        }
    })();





    $(document).ready(function () {






        function syncPeopleCharacter3d(swiper) {
            var fallbackName = 'quokka';
            var character3d = document.querySelector('.people_sect .character_3d');
            if (!swiper || !character3d || !swiper.slides || typeof swiper.activeIndex === 'undefined') return;

            var activeSlide = swiper.slides[swiper.activeIndex];
            var requestedName = activeSlide ? activeSlide.getAttribute('data-name') : '';
            var imageTarget = requestedName ? character3d.querySelector('.img img[data-name="' + requestedName + '"]') : null;
            var boxTarget = requestedName ? character3d.querySelector('.tit_box .box[data-name="' + requestedName + '"]') : null;
            var activeName = imageTarget || boxTarget ? requestedName : fallbackName;

            character3d.querySelectorAll('.img img.active, .tit_box .box.active').forEach(function (el) {
                el.classList.remove('active');
            });

            ['.img img', '.tit_box .box'].forEach(function (selector) {
                var target = character3d.querySelector(selector + '[data-name="' + activeName + '"]');
                if (target) {
                    target.classList.add('active');
                }
            });
        }

        window.initialPeopleCaseSwiperRealIndex = null;
        window.peopleCaseSwiperInitStarted = false;
        window.peopleCaseSwiperInitPending = false;
        window.peopleCaseSwiperFontsReady = !document.fonts || !document.fonts.ready;
        window.peopleCaseSwiperVisible = false;
        window.peopleCaseSwiperVisibilityObserver = null;
        window.peopleCaseSwiperResizeRaf = null;

        function runPeopleCaseSwiperAfterFrames(callback) {
            requestAnimationFrame(function () {
                requestAnimationFrame(callback);
            });
        }

        function settlePeopleCaseSwiper(swiper) {
            runPeopleCaseSwiperAfterFrames(function () {
                if (!swiper || swiper.destroyed) return;

                swiper.update();
                swiper.loopFix();
                var settledSlideIndex = getPeopleCasePreferredSlideIndex(
                    swiper,
                    typeof swiper.realIndex === 'number' ? swiper.realIndex : 0
                );
                swiper.slideTo(settledSlideIndex > -1 ? settledSlideIndex : swiper.activeIndex, 0, false);

                window.initialPeopleCaseSwiperRealIndex =
                    typeof swiper.realIndex === 'number'
                        ? swiper.realIndex
                        : 0;

                syncPeopleCharacter3d(swiper);

                 
                 
                 
                if (typeof window.recalibratePeopleMoveLanding === 'function') {
                    window.recalibratePeopleMoveLanding();
                }
            });
        }

        function getPeopleCasePreferredSlideIndex(swiper, realIndex) {
            if (!swiper || !swiper.slides || typeof realIndex !== 'number') return -1;

            for (var i = 0; i < swiper.slides.length; i++) {
                var slideEl = swiper.slides[i];
                if (!slideEl || slideEl.classList.contains('swiper-slide-duplicate')) continue;

                var slideRealIndex = parseInt(slideEl.getAttribute('data-swiper-slide-index'), 10);
                if (slideRealIndex === realIndex) {
                    return i;
                }
            }

            return -1;
        }

        function destroyPeopleCaseSwiper() {
            if (!window.caseSwiper || window.caseSwiper.destroyed) {
                window.caseSwiper = null;
                window.peopleCaseSwiperInitStarted = false;
                return;
            }

            if (typeof window.caseSwiper.realIndex === 'number') {
                window.initialPeopleCaseSwiperRealIndex = window.caseSwiper.realIndex;
            }

            window.caseSwiper.destroy(true, true);
            window.caseSwiper = null;
            window.peopleCaseSwiperInitStarted = false;
        }

        function rebuildPeopleCaseSwiper() {
            if (window.innerWidth > 1040) return;
            if (!window.peopleCaseSwiperFontsReady) return;

            destroyPeopleCaseSwiper();

            runPeopleCaseSwiperAfterFrames(function () {
                if (!(window.peopleCaseSwiperVisible || isPeopleCaseSwiperVisibleReady())) return;
                initPeopleCaseSwiper();
            });
        }

        function refreshPeopleCaseSwiperLayout() {
            if (window.innerWidth > 1040) return;

            if (window.peopleCaseSwiperResizeRaf) {
                cancelAnimationFrame(window.peopleCaseSwiperResizeRaf);
            }

            window.peopleCaseSwiperResizeRaf = requestAnimationFrame(function () {
                window.peopleCaseSwiperResizeRaf = requestAnimationFrame(function () {
                    rebuildPeopleCaseSwiper();
                    window.peopleCaseSwiperResizeRaf = null;
                });
            });
        }

        function isPeopleCaseSwiperVisibleReady() {
            var slideEl = document.querySelector('.people_sect .slide');
            if (!slideEl) return false;
            if (slideEl.classList.contains('on')) return true;

            var rect = slideEl.getBoundingClientRect();
            var viewportHeight = window.innerHeight || document.documentElement.clientHeight || 0;

            return rect.top < viewportHeight * 1.2 && rect.bottom > viewportHeight * -0.2;
        }

        window.slidePeopleCaseSwiperToInitial = function () {


            if (!window.caseSwiper) return;

            if (typeof window.initialPeopleCaseSwiperRealIndex === 'number') {
                var preferredSlideIndex = getPeopleCasePreferredSlideIndex(
                    window.caseSwiper,
                    window.initialPeopleCaseSwiperRealIndex
                );

                if (preferredSlideIndex > -1) {
                    window.caseSwiper.slideTo(preferredSlideIndex, 0, false);
                } else if (typeof window.caseSwiper.slideToLoop === 'function') {
                    window.caseSwiper.slideToLoop(window.initialPeopleCaseSwiperRealIndex, 0, false);
                }
            }

            syncPeopleCharacter3d(window.caseSwiper);
        };

        function initPeopleCaseSwiper() {
            if (window.caseSwiper || window.peopleCaseSwiperInitStarted) return;

            var peopleSwiperEl = document.querySelector('.people_sect .swiper-container');
            if (!peopleSwiperEl || typeof Swiper === 'undefined') return;
            var rootFontSize = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).fontSize) || 10;
            var peopleSwipeStartX = 0;
            var peopleSwipeMoved = false;
            var peopleSwipeThreshold = 24;
            var peopleSlideCount = peopleSwiperEl.querySelectorAll('.swiper-slide:not(.swiper-slide-duplicate)').length;

            function slidePeopleCaseBy(swiper, step) {
                if (!swiper || swiper.destroyed || !peopleSlideCount) return;

                var currentRealIndex = typeof swiper.realIndex === 'number' ? swiper.realIndex : 0;
                var targetRealIndex = (currentRealIndex + step + peopleSlideCount) % peopleSlideCount;
                var activeIndex = typeof swiper.activeIndex === 'number' ? swiper.activeIndex : 0;
                var targetSlideIndex = -1;

                if (swiper.slides && swiper.slides.length) {
                    for (var i = 0; i < swiper.slides.length; i++) {
                        var slideEl = swiper.slides[i];
                        if (!slideEl) continue;

                        var slideRealIndex = parseInt(slideEl.getAttribute('data-swiper-slide-index'), 10);
                        if (slideRealIndex !== targetRealIndex) continue;

                        if (step > 0 && i > activeIndex) {
                            targetSlideIndex = i;
                            break;
                        }

                        if (step < 0 && i < activeIndex) {
                            targetSlideIndex = i;
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (targetSlideIndex > -1) {
                    swiper.slideTo(targetSlideIndex, swiper.params.speed, true);
                    return;
                }

                if (typeof swiper.slideToLoop === 'function') {
                    swiper.slideToLoop(targetRealIndex, swiper.params.speed, true);
                    return;
                }

                if (step > 0) {
                    swiper.slideNext();
                } else {
                    swiper.slidePrev();
                }
            }

            window.peopleCaseSwiperInitStarted = true;
            window.caseSwiper = new Swiper(peopleSwiperEl, {
                slidesPerView: 'auto',
                slidesPerGroup: 1,
                slidesPerGroupAuto: false,
                spaceBetween: rootFontSize * 8,
                loop: true,
                speed: 750,
                centeredSlides: true,
                followFinger: false,
                longSwipes: false,
                longSwipesRatio: 0.5,
                longSwipesMs: 300,
                shortSwipes: false,
                resistanceRatio: 0,
                threshold: 8,
                loopedSlides: $('.people_sect .swiper-container .swiper-slide').length,
                loopAdditionalSlides: 2,

                breakpoints: {
                    1025: {
                        slidesPerView: 'auto',
                        spaceBetween: rootFontSize * 8
                    },
                    600: {
                        slidesPerView: 5,
                        spaceBetween: 40
                    },
                    0: {
                        slidesPerView: 4.5,
                        spaceBetween: 25
                    },
                },


                 

                on: {
                    init: function () {
                        settlePeopleCaseSwiper(this);
                    },
                    touchStart: function () {
                        peopleSwipeStartX = this.touches && typeof this.touches.startX === 'number'
                            ? this.touches.startX
                            : 0;
                        peopleSwipeMoved = false;
                    },
                    touchMove: function () {
                        peopleSwipeMoved = true;
                    },
                    touchEnd: function () {
                        var currentX = this.touches && typeof this.touches.currentX === 'number'
                            ? this.touches.currentX
                            : peopleSwipeStartX;
                        var deltaX = currentX - peopleSwipeStartX;

                        if (!peopleSwipeMoved || Math.abs(deltaX) < peopleSwipeThreshold) return;

                        if (deltaX < 0) {
                            slidePeopleCaseBy(this, 1);
                        } else {
                            slidePeopleCaseBy(this, -1);
                        }
                    },

                    slideChangeTransitionStart: function () {
                        $('.people_sect .swiper-container .swiper-slide')
                            .addClass('changing')
                            .removeClass('changed');

                        syncPeopleCharacter3d(this);
                    },

                    slideChangeTransitionEnd: function () {
                        $('.people_sect .swiper-container .swiper-slide')
                            .removeClass('changing')
                            .addClass('changed');

                         
                         
                         
                        if (typeof window.recalibratePeopleMoveLanding === 'function') {
                            window.recalibratePeopleMoveLanding();
                        }

                        if (window.innerWidth <= 1040 && this.activeIndex > -1) {
                            var activeSlide = this.slides && this.slides[this.activeIndex];
                            if (activeSlide && activeSlide.classList.contains('swiper-slide-duplicate')) {
                                var normalizedSlideIndex = getPeopleCasePreferredSlideIndex(this, this.realIndex);
                                if (normalizedSlideIndex > -1 && normalizedSlideIndex !== this.activeIndex) {
                                    this.slideTo(normalizedSlideIndex, 0, false);
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        syncPeopleCharacter3d(this);
                    },
                },
            });
        }

         

        window.requestPeopleCaseSwiperInit = function () {
            if (window.caseSwiper || window.peopleCaseSwiperInitStarted || window.peopleCaseSwiperInitPending) return;
            if (!window.peopleCaseSwiperFontsReady) return;
            if (!(window.peopleCaseSwiperVisible || isPeopleCaseSwiperVisibleReady())) return;

            window.peopleCaseSwiperInitPending = true;

            runPeopleCaseSwiperAfterFrames(function () {
                window.peopleCaseSwiperInitPending = false;

                if (window.caseSwiper || window.peopleCaseSwiperInitStarted) return;
                if (!(window.peopleCaseSwiperVisible || isPeopleCaseSwiperVisibleReady())) return;

                initPeopleCaseSwiper();
            });
        };

        (function initPeopleCaseSwiperVisibilityObserver() {
            var peopleSect = document.querySelector('.people_sect');
            if (!peopleSect || typeof IntersectionObserver === 'undefined') return;

            window.peopleCaseSwiperVisibilityObserver = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
                entries.forEach(function (entry) {
                    if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;

                    window.peopleCaseSwiperVisible = true;
                    window.requestPeopleCaseSwiperInit();

                    if (window.peopleCaseSwiperVisibilityObserver) {
                        window.peopleCaseSwiperVisibilityObserver.disconnect();
                        window.peopleCaseSwiperVisibilityObserver = null;
                    }
                });
            }, {
                rootMargin: '20% 0px 20% 0px',
                threshold: 0.01
            });

            window.peopleCaseSwiperVisibilityObserver.observe(peopleSect);
        })();

        if (document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) {
            document.fonts.ready.then(function () {
                window.peopleCaseSwiperFontsReady = true;

                if (window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh) {
                    window.scheduleMainScrollTriggerRefresh([0, 120]);
                }

                window.requestPeopleCaseSwiperInit();
            });
        }

        


        if (window.onLayoutResize) {
            window.onLayoutResize(refreshPeopleCaseSwiperLayout);
        } else {
            window.addEventListener('resize', refreshPeopleCaseSwiperLayout);
            window.addEventListener('orientationchange', refreshPeopleCaseSwiperLayout);
        }

        function contObserver(selector, num, type, moChk, slide, onEnter) {
            const isMobile = window.innerWidth < 768;

            if (isMobile && !moChk) {
                return;
            }

            const threshold = isMobile && moChk !== true ? moChk : num;

            const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
                entries.forEach((entry) => {
                    const $target = entry.target;
                    const isIntersecting = entry.isIntersecting;

                    if (isIntersecting) {
                        $($target).addClass("active");
                        if (onEnter) {
                            onEnter($target);
                        }
                        if (slide) {
                            slide.autoplay.start();
                        }

                    } else if (type) {
                        $($target).removeClass("active");
                    }
                });
            }, { threshold: threshold });

            document.querySelectorAll(selector).forEach((item) => {
                observer.observe(item);
            });
        }

        contObserver('.band_slide_cont', 0.1, false, 0.1);
        contObserver('.review_sect .since2_cont', 0.5, false, 0.3);
        contObserver('.pickup_sect .img_box', 0.3, false, 0.1);
        contObserver('.order_now_sect .slide', 0.5, false, 0.3);
        contObserver('.made_sect .video', 0.5, false, 0.3);
        contObserver('.character_sect .portfolio_character', 0.5, false, 0.3);
        // contObserver('.character_sect .character_cont', 0.5, false, 0.3);
        contObserver('.portfolio_sect .portfolio_wrap .award_cont', 0.2, false, 0.2, null, function ($target) {
            var target = $target;

            if (target.classList.contains('is-award-enter-complete')) return;

            target.classList.add('is-award-entering');
            target.awardEnterTimer = setTimeout(function () {
                target.classList.add('is-award-enter-complete');
                target.classList.remove('is-award-entering');
            }, 1300);

        });

         
         


        (function initAwardTrophyVideoObserver() {
            var awardCont = document.querySelector('.portfolio_sect .portfolio_wrap .award_cont');
            var trophy = awardCont ? awardCont.querySelector('.trophy') : null;
            var video = trophy ? trophy.querySelector('.item') : null;
            if (!awardCont || !trophy || !video) return;

            function playVideo() {
                video.muted = true;
                video.loop = false;
                video.playsInline = true;
                video.preload = 'auto';
                var playPromise = video.play();
                if (playPromise && playPromise.catch) {
                    playPromise.catch(function () { });
                }
            }

            function resetVideo() {
                video.pause();
                try {
                    video.currentTime = 0;
                } catch (e) { }
            }

            if (!('IntersectionObserver' in window)) {
                playVideo();
                return;
            }

            var inView = false;

            new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
                entries.forEach(function (entry) {
                    inView = entry.isIntersecting && entry.intersectionRatio >= 0.5;
                    if (inView) {
                        if (!document.hidden) playVideo();
                    } else {
                        resetVideo();
                    }
                });
            }, {
                threshold: [0, 0.5]
            }).observe(trophy);

            document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () {
                if (document.hidden) {
                    video.pause();
                } else if (inView) {
                    playVideo();
                }
            });
        })();

        //         }

        //     if (!('IntersectionObserver' in window)) {

        (function initPortfolioCharacterVideoObserver() {
            var video = document.querySelector('.character_sect .portfolio_character .item');
            var isPrimed = false;
            var isInView = false;
            var hasPlayedOnce = false;
            if (!video) return;

            function playVideo() {
                if (document.hidden) return;
                video.muted = true;
                video.playsInline = true;
                video.preload = 'auto';
                var playPromise = video.play();
                if (playPromise && playPromise.catch) {
                    playPromise.catch(function () {   });
                }
            }

            function holdPoster() {
                video.pause();
                try {
                    video.currentTime = 0;
                } catch (e) { }
                video.preload = 'none';
                video.load();
            }

            function primeVideo() {
                if (isPrimed) return;
                isPrimed = true;
                holdPoster();
            }

            function resetVideo() {
                hasPlayedOnce = false;
                isPrimed = false;
                holdPoster();
            }

            function playIfReady() {
                 
                if (window.portfolioCharacterVideoScrubActive || window.portfolioCharacterVideoTimelineMode) return;
                playVideo();
            }

            function playOnceIfReady() {
                if (window.portfolioCharacterVideoScrubActive) return false;
                if (!isInView || document.hidden || hasPlayedOnce) return false;
                hasPlayedOnce = true;
                playVideo();
                return true;
            }

            function bindVideoObserver() {
                if (!('IntersectionObserver' in window)) {
                    isInView = true;
                    if (!window.portfolioCharacterVideoTimelineMode) {
                        playIfReady();
                    }
                    return;
                }

                new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
                    entries.forEach(function (entry) {
                        isInView = entry.isIntersecting;

                        if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                            if (!window.portfolioCharacterVideoTimelineMode) {
                                playIfReady();
                            }
                        } else {
                            video.pause();
                        }
                    });
                }, {
                    threshold: 0.35
                }).observe(video);
            }

            document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () {
                if (document.hidden) {
                    video.pause();
                } else if (isInView) {
                    if (!window.portfolioCharacterVideoTimelineMode) {
                        playIfReady();
                    }
                }
            });

            window.portfolioCharacterVideoController = {
                prime: primeVideo,
                playIfReady: playIfReady,
                playOnceIfReady: playOnceIfReady,
                reset: resetVideo
            };

            primeVideo();
            bindVideoObserver();
        })();


        (function initPortfolioResetButton() {
            var resetBtn = document.querySelector('.character_sect .character_wrap .sect_tit_box .reset_btn');
            var loadingBg = document.querySelector('.portfolio_sect .loading_bg');
            var stickerRootSelector = '.character_wrap[data-area="sticker"], .character_wrap [data-area="sticker"], .portfolio_sect[data-area="sticker"], .portfolio_sect [data-area="sticker"]';
            var isResetting = false;

            if (!resetBtn || !loadingBg) return;

            function clearClickStickers() {
                var stickerRoot = document.querySelector(stickerRootSelector);
                if (!stickerRoot) return;

                if (window.portfolioStickerBoard && typeof window.portfolioStickerBoard.clear === 'function') {
                    window.portfolioStickerBoard.clear(stickerRoot);
                    return;
                }

                Array.prototype.slice.call(stickerRoot.children).forEach(function (child) {
                    if (!child.classList.contains('sticker-box')) return;
                    stickerRoot.removeChild(child);
                });
            }

            resetBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
                if (isResetting) return;

                isResetting = true;
                loadingBg.classList.remove('is-loading', 'is-exit', 'is-fadeout');
                loadingBg.classList.add('is-active');

                requestAnimationFrame(function () {
                    loadingBg.classList.add('is-loading');
                });

                setTimeout(function () {
                    clearClickStickers();
                    loadingBg.classList.add('is-exit');
                }, 550);

                setTimeout(function () {
                    loadingBg.classList.add('is-fadeout');
                }, 1200);

                setTimeout(function () {
                    loadingBg.classList.remove('is-active', 'is-loading', 'is-exit', 'is-fadeout');
                    isResetting = false;
                }, 1450);
            });
        })();


    })</script><footer class="footer" data-sect="footerSect">

        scrollTrigger: {
            trigger: '.footer',
            start: 'top bottom',
            end: 'bottom 100%',
